### [26] [AutoResearch at Production Scale: Failure Modes and a Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30541)
*Aparajith Chandran,Juwon Kim,Saurav Jha,Pablo Castells,Florian Hottier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在图书推荐生产 pipeline 上规模化运行 LLM AutoResearch 范式 12 周、220+ 实验，发现五类生产级失败模式，并提出 prevent/persist/redirect 三原则 scaffolding。框架取得 Recall@6 1.82x、coherence 2.1x 提升，并让 agent 自主设计 text-only fallback 使目录覆盖扩大 5.8x，对推荐系统自动化实验与 LLM agent 生产化具有较强参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产规模推荐 pipeline 中，用 LLM 自动迭代编辑训练脚本并保留提升 held-out 标量指标的修改这一 AutoResearch 范式，会因迭代成本高、评估标准竞争、campaign 跨周跨多训练任务而出现原设定中不存在的系统性失败模式，需要结构性 scaffolding 才能稳定运行。

Method: 输入为生产推荐 pipeline 中的训练脚本与 held-out 标量评估指标；LLM agent 迭代编辑训练脚本，运行多 GPU 训练，评估后保留提升指标的修改，否则回退。系统在两周以上 campaign 中持续运行，并管理多个训练任务。论文观察到基础设施脆弱、记忆衰减、搜索方向停滞、迭代成本不对称、指标固着五类失败模式，提出 prevent、persist、redirect 三原则 scaffolding：prevent 通过结构性约束避免已知故障，persist 通过持久化状态对抗 agent 记忆衰减，redirect 通过重定向搜索方向避免停滞与指标固着；scaffolding 实例化随迭代成本缩放。两类独立表示学习系统均采用该框架，其中 agent 还自主设计了 text-only fallback 以扩展目录覆盖。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Recall@6", "value": "1.82x lift over hand-tuned baselines", "setting": "图书推荐 pipeline 的表示学习系统，生产规模 12 周运行、220+ 实验，两类系统之一或整体结果"}, {"metric": "coherence", "value": "2.1x lift over hand-tuned baselines", "setting": "图书推荐 pipeline 的表示学习系统，生产规模 12 周运行、220+ 实验，两类系统之一或整体结果"}, {"metric": "catalog coverage", "value": "5.8x expansion", "setting": "agent 自主设计的 text-only fallback 相对基线"}]}

Conclusion: 将 Karpathy 的 AutoResearch 范式扩展到生产规模多 GPU、多小时迭代、跨周 campaign 的推荐嵌入优化场景。；识别出五类反复出现的失败模式：infrastructure fragility、agent memory decay、search-direction stagnation、iteration-cost asymmetry、metric fixation。；提出三原则 scaffolding 设计 prevent、persist、redirect，将每个失败模式映射到结构性补救措施，并使其实例化随迭代成本伸缩。；在两类独立开发的表示学习系统上验证失败模式的结构性，覆盖单次迭代成本相差近三个数量级的系统。；智能体自主设计 text-only fallback，显著扩展目录覆盖。

Abstract: Optimizing embedding systems for production recommendation pipelines demands systematic exploration that consumes disproportionate engineering effort at scale. We apply Andrej Karpathy's AutoResearch paradigm -- a large language model that iteratively edits a training script and retains modifications that improve a held-out scalar metric -- to automate this exploration. We report on twelve weeks of running this paradigm at production scale, where iterations consume hours of multi-GPU compute, evaluation involves competing criteria, and campaigns span weeks across many training jobs. Across two independently developed representation-learning systems for a book recommendation pipeline, we ran 220+ experiments and observed five recurring failure modes absent from the original setting: infrastructure fragility, agent memory decay, search-direction stagnation, iteration-cost asymmetry, and metric fixation. We contribute a three-principle scaffolding design -- prevent, persist, redirect -- that maps each failure mode to a structural remedy and whose instantiation scales with iteration cost. The framework produced a 1.82x Recall@6 lift and a 2.1x coherence lift over hand-tuned baselines, and the agent autonomously designed a text-only fallback that expanded catalog coverage by 5.8x. The two systems span nearly three orders of magnitude in per-iteration cost yet exhibit the same failure modes, suggesting these are structural properties of production-scale autonomous research rather than artifacts of either application.

</details>


### [27] [Cost-Aware Best-LLM Identification using Dueling Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30360)
*Sarvesh Gharat,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向异质成本LLM集合的最优模型识别问题，提出带对决反馈与查询成本的多臂老虎机算法，理论证明渐近最优成本，并在真实数据集上验证，对推荐系统模型路由与在线实验有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在查询成本异质的多个LLM中，利用成对比较反馈，以给定置信度识别最优模型，同时最小化总查询成本。

Method: 论文提出一种Track-and-Stop风格的最佳臂识别算法。输入为多个LLM，每个模型有异质查询成本；反馈为对决式成对比较。算法维护每个模型成为最优的置信区间或经验估计，通过求解一个成本感知的最优分配问题确定各模型应被采样的比例，然后以追踪方式逐步采样，当某个模型的置信上界/下界分离满足预设置信度时停止并输出最优模型。理论证明在Condorcet赢家存在且误差趋于零时，总成本渐近最优。实验在合成与真实数据集上运行，与经典成本无关算法及成本感知扩展进行对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将异质采样成本与对决反馈结合，形式化为带成本的对决老虎机最佳臂识别问题；在Condorcet赢家假设下提出Track-and-Stop风格算法，实现渐近最优成本；在多个真实数据集上验证Condorcet赢家假设，并实证相比成本无关及成本感知扩展算法的改进

Abstract: Inspired by the problem of identifying the best model from a collection of large language models (LLMs) with heterogeneous querying costs, we formulate and analyse a variant of the multi-armed bandit (MAB) with (i) dueling feedback, where pairwise comparisons between model responses provide robust preference signals, and (ii) heterogeneous sampling costs, reflecting the differing costs of querying different LLMs. Assuming the existence of a Condorcet winner, a condition we empirically validate across multiple real-world datasets, we propose a Track-and-Stop style algorithm for best-arm identification with prescribed confidence. We prove that the algorithm almost surely achieves the asymptotically optimal cost as the error tends to zero. Finally, we extensively evaluate our approach on both synthetic and real-world instances, demonstrating consistent improvements over classical cost-unaware algorithms and their cost-aware extensions.

</details>


### [28] [Strategic Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30352)
*Tori Qiu,Ander Artola Velasco,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示self-consistency推理中按路径计费的经济激励可被不诚实提供商利用：通过生成和重排额外推理路径，可避免被审计发现而过度计费。在Llama、Qwen及DeepSeek-R1蒸馏模型上的实验表明额外路径分布厚尾，且即使假阳性率约束下审计仍难以检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在self-consistency推理中，按路径数量计费导致不诚实提供商可通过策略性生成和重排额外推理路径来制造路径必要性的假象，从而过度计费且难以被审计检测。

Method: 论文架构围绕self-consistency推理流程展开：输入为问题提示，模型生成多条推理路径，然后通过多数投票选择最终答案。不诚实的提供商在生成阶段额外插入推理路径，并通过策略性重排序使这些额外路径在路径集合中扮演关键角色，从而在任何被审计的子集上都显得必要。审计方则尝试以假阳性率不超过α=0.1的条件检测异常。实验使用Llama、Qwen系列instruct模型以及从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏的推理模型，在数学、科学和问答基准数据集上进行，分析了额外路径数量分布的厚尾特性。训练目标、优化方法和推理硬件资源未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一种简单高效的算法，通过生成并策略性重排序额外推理路径，使所有路径看起来都是达到多数投票所必需的；设计实验验证该攻击在多个模型和数据集上的有效性，并分析额外路径分布的厚尾特性；在假阳性率约束（α=0.1）下评估最佳审计的漏检情况，表明过度计费容量仍然显著

Abstract: Self-consistency has become a popular technique for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models by generating multiple reasoning paths and selecting the final answer through a majority vote. However, because model providers typically charge users in proportion to the number of reasoning paths generated, they have a financial incentive to artificially increase the path count. In this work, we show that an unfaithful provider can exploit this incentive using a simple, efficient algorithm while avoiding detection by an auditor: by generating and strategically reordering additional reasoning paths, the algorithm makes every path appear necessary to reach the majority. To validate our algorithm, we conduct experiments with multiple instruct models from the Llama and Qwen families, as well as reasoning models distilled from DeepSeek-R1, on benchmark datasets spanning mathematics, science, and question answering. Our results suggest that the distribution of additional reasoning paths generated by our algorithm is heavy-tailed and that substantial capacity to overcharge remains even under the best possible audit designed to keep the false-positive rate below $\alpha = 0.1$.

</details>


### [29] [LUMO (Lightweight Unified Multilingual Orchestrator): A Privacy Preserving Offline Voice Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30692)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Mst. Kamrunnahar Ruma,Nafiza Anjum,Shakila Sultana,Md. Sujan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUMO is a fully offline, privacy-preserving voice assistant running on a Raspberry Pi 5 (8 GB RAM), combining local ASR, a 4-bit GGUF quantized LLM, and local TTS. It reports 6.8% WER for short English utterances, 2.0-4.0 s end-to-end latency, and ~9.0 W peak power, with multilingual Bangla support, targeting edge scenarios like rural healthcare and disaster response.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enabling reliable, low-latency, privacy-preserving spoken interaction on resource-constrained edge hardware without cloud dependency, by orchestrating local ASR, a quantized generative LLM, and local TTS in a fully offline pipeline.

Method: LUMO is a fully offline voice assistant pipeline designed for edge execution on a Raspberry Pi 5 with 8 GB RAM. The input is user speech, which is first processed by a local Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) module to produce text. The recognized text is then passed to a locally deployed Large Language Model (LLM) that has been compressed using 4-bit GGUF quantization, enabling it to run within the memory constraints of the device while retaining practical conversational ability. The LLM generates a text response, which is converted to speech by a local Text-to-Speech (TTS) synthesis module. These three modules form an end-to-end orchestrated pipeline that never requires cloud connectivity, preserving privacy and eliminating network latency. The paper reports system-level evaluation: WER of 6.8% for short English utterances in low noise, end-to-end latency of 2.0-4.0 s, and peak power consumption of approximately 9.0 W. It also notes effective offline recognition for Bangla speech. The design prioritizes low memory usage via quantization and suitability for privacy-sensitive edge scenarios such as rural healthcare, education, and disaster response.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Word Error Rate (WER)", "value": "6.8%", "setting": "short English utterances, low noise conditions"}, {"metric": "end-to-end response latency", "value": "2.0-4.0 s", "setting": "fully offline pipeline on Raspberry Pi 5 with 8 GB RAM"}, {"metric": "peak power consumption", "value": "~9.0 W", "setting": "system-level peak power on Raspberry Pi 5"}, {"metric": "memory usage", "value": "not quantified; described as reduced via 4-bit GGUF quantization", "setting": "quantized local LLM deployment on 8 GB RAM device"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Unified offline voice assistant pipeline integrating local ASR, locally deployed quantized LLM, and local TTS on a Raspberry Pi 5 (8 GB RAM).；Use of 4-bit GGUF quantization to compress the LLM for memory-constrained edge hardware while preserving practical conversational capability.；Reported end-to-end system trade-offs (WER, latency, power) against existing edge voice assistants (Mycroft, Rhasspy), including multilingual Bangla speech recognition support.

Abstract: Reliable voice interaction is essential in environments with limited internet connectivity and strong privacy. However, most existing voice assistants depend on cloud-based services, which leads to latency issues, dependency on internet access, and privacy vulnerabilities. This research presents LUMO (Lightweight Unified Multilingual Orchestrator), a privacy preserving offline voice assistant designed for edge computing environments. This system integrates local Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), locally deployed quantized Large Language Model (LLM), and Text-to-Speech (TTS) synthesis into a fully offline pipeline running on a Raspberry Pi 5 with 8 GB RAM. To enable efficient operation on resource constrained hardware, the language model is compressed using 4-bit GGUF quantization, which reduces memory usage while preserving practical conversational capability. Existing edge based voice assistants Mycroft provides partial offline functionality without a generative LLM, with an approximate latency of ~5 s and power consumption of ~12 W, while Rhasspy supports full offline operation but lacks generative capabilities, with ~3 s latency and ~11 W power usage. In contrast, LUMO achieves a Word Error Rate (WER) of 6.8% for short English utterances in low noise conditions, an end-to-end response latency of 2.0-4.0 s, and a lower peak power consumption of approximately 9.0 W. The system also achieves effective offline recognition for Bangla speech, supporting multilingual accessibility in low resource settings. By operating entirely offline, LUMO provides strong data privacy, reduced need for cloud connectivity, and suitability for privacy sensitive edge execution such as rural healthcare, education, and disaster response scenarios.

</details>


### [30] [New LoRA Skills Should Read but Never Write](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31600)
*Zeyan Li,Panqi Yang,Qirong Guo,Shengda Zhuo,SIyuan Qiu,Hu Xu,Chun Li,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: READ 通过将 LoRA 适配器重写为平衡规范形式并采用单向耦合，使新技能能读取旧技能输入而不能写入其输出，从而在无推理开销下将多个独立 LoRA 适配器组合为单一模型，在 SuperGLUE 和领域套件上分别平均提升超 20 点和 7 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将多个独立训练的 LoRA 适配器组合成一个单一模型，同时避免权重空间合并导致的干扰、全任务数据重训练的高成本以及多适配器路由带来的额外推理开销。

Method: READ 的端到端架构如下：输入为多个独立训练的 LoRA 适配器及基础模型权重。首先，每个 LoRA 适配器被重写为平衡规范形式（balanced canonical form），该变换保持适配器更新完全不变，但固定了因子分解中的自由度，使后续耦合学习能够明确感知到因子坐标。然后，在组合多个适配器时，采用单向耦合机制：当添加新技能时，新技能的适配器与已有适配器之间的耦合矩阵仅在新技能的行上可训练，其方向设定为旧技能的输入子空间可被新技能读取，但新技能不能写入旧技能的输出子空间，从而防止旧技能行为被破坏。训练目标仅优化新技能的耦合行，保持旧适配器参数冻结。训练完成后，所有适配器的更新和耦合被折叠（folding）到基础模型权重中，形成单一组合模型。推理时无需路由、任务特定规则或额外计算开销，直接使用折叠后的基础模型。该设计解决了组合多个适配器时因子分解不唯一和耦合方向不明确导致的干扰问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "SuperGLUE 平均提升", "value": "超过最强基线 20 点以上", "setting": "在多个模型家族上，逐个添加技能，与基于相同适配器的最强已发表基线对比"}, {"metric": "领域套件平均提升", "value": "超过最强基线 7 点以上", "setting": "在多个模型家族上，逐个添加技能，与基于相同适配器的最强已发表基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 READ（Read-only Expansion of Adapter Deltas），通过将每个适配器改写为平衡规范形式（balanced canonical form）来固定因子分解选择，保留原始更新不变。；引入单向耦合（one-directional coupling），使新技能能够读取旧技能的输入子空间但不能写入其输出子空间，从而保护旧技能的计算结果。；每次追加时仅训练新技能在耦合矩阵中的行，组合后的更新折叠进基础权重，实现无推理成本、无路由或任务特定规则的组合模型。

Abstract: Low-rank adapters (LoRA) make it cheap to fine-tune a large language model once per task, but combining several independently trained adapters into one model remains difficult: merging the updates in weight space causes interference, retraining on all task data is expensive, and routing between separate adapters gives up the goal of a single combined model. We trace the difficulty to two choices that every composition method makes implicitly. A LoRA update admits infinitely many equivalent factorizations; the choice among them is invisible while an adapter serves alone, but it determines what a learned interaction between adapters can see. A coupling between an old skill and a new one can likewise point in either direction, and the direction decides whether the old skills keep computing what they computed before. We introduce READ (Read-only Expansion of Adapter Deltas), which fixes both choices: each adapter is rewritten into a balanced canonical form that preserves its update exactly, and the coupling grows in one direction only, so a new skill can read the input subspaces of old skills but cannot write into their output subspaces. The only trainable object at each append is the new skill's row of the coupling matrix, and the composed update folds into the base weights with no inference cost, routing, or task-specific rules. We evaluate READ across four benchmark suites and two model families, adding skills one at a time. Across several families, READ improves every suite average over the strongest published baselines built from the same adapters---by more than twenty points on SuperGLUE and more than seven points on the domain suite---and nearly all complete addition sequences end above every direct baseline. Factor coordinates and coupling direction, which a lone adapter never exposes, are what decide whether composed skills survive.

</details>

*Erin George,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 理论分析OPTQ量化算法的泛化误差界，并给出正则化参数λ的新选择建议，实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPTQ量化算法在泛化场景下的误差界以及正则化参数λ的选择对量化后模型性能的影响。

Method: 论文未描述具体模型架构，主要分析OPTQ量化算法的泛化性质。OPTQ逐步量化神经网络权重，使在校准数据集上的平方量化误差最小化。文中定义了随机OPTQ变体，并利用概率工具推导期望平方误差的泛化界。理论结果涉及校准数据集独立同分布采样时的误差界，以及正则化项λ的作用。实验部分比较了不同λ选择的量化性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导了OPTQ在测试点固定分布下的期望平方误差泛化界，将泛化误差与校准数据集误差关联。；推导了随机OPTQ在任意足够好分布下、不依赖校准数据集的泛化误差界。；基于理论分析给出正则化参数λ的新选择建议，并在实验中验证优于先前推荐。

Abstract: Large neural networks can be compressed by rounding or "quantizing" their weights to numbers that admit representations with fewer bits. One algorithm for quantization, OPTQ, progressively quantizes the weights of a neural network so that the squared quantization error on a specified calibration dataset is as small as possible. We study the performance of OPTQ and a variant algorithm, stochastic OPTQ, in a generalization setting and derive bounds for the expected squared error accrued by the algorithm when a test point is drawn from a fixed distribution. We prove two results. One result relates the generalization error to the error on a calibration dataset comprising independent samples from the same distribution as the test distribution. The other result bounds the generalization error of stochastic OPTQ for all sufficiently nice distributions, regardless of the calibration dataset. In both of these results, the regularization term $\lambda$ plays an important role. We use insights from these results to make a new recommendation for the choice of $\lambda$ and see that this choice of $\lambda$ preforms favorably in experiments when compared to prior recommendations in the literature.

</details>

Conclusion: 跨公共预训练与后训练检查点跟踪probe准确率、模型输出和steering响应；使用保存的分数区分probe-correct错误中正确候选概率低与高于均匀分布两种情况；比较在最终状态或候选logits上训练的解码器，未发现最终状态在晚期检查点模型错误上具有优势；给出信息论反例，说明仅凭错误上的可解码性无法确立输出信息被丢弃

Abstract: Prior work established that a probe can decode an in-context binding on model errors and that probe-guided steering can repair some of them. We follow probe accuracy, model output, and steering response across public pretraining and post-training checkpoints. Probe accuracy rises during Pythia pretraining, while probe-guided steering moves from negligible all-trial benefit to a larger benefit at two model sizes. Saved scores distinguish probe-correct errors with low and above-uniform model probability for the correct candidate. Oracle-target steering already repairs many early errors, but saved aggregates cannot separate target quality from intervention sensitivity. A held-out comparison of decoders trained on the final state or candidate logits finds no detected final-state advantage on late-checkpoint model errors. An information-theoretic counterexample explains why decodability on errors alone cannot establish discarded output information. The connection to downstream omissions remains open.

</details>

Abstract: Position-independent KV cache reuse aims to reduce latency in retrieval-augmented generation by reusing chunk-level KV caches across prompts. We show that current evaluations of KV cache reuse techniques rely on measurements that fail to faithfully capture the loss of accuracy attributable to reuse, often artificially inflating the reported effectiveness. We also show that existing datasets do not exhibit the reuse dynamics needed to thoroughly evaluate such techniques. To address these issues, we propose an evaluation methodology that measures this accuracy loss without ambiguity and we introduce Boxoffice, a tool that programmatically generates evaluation datasets that exercise challenging KV cache reuse patterns.

</details>


### [34] [Benchmarking Attention for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31306)
*Maximilian Schambach,Clemens Biehl,Sam Thelin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格基础模型（如 TabPFN、Mitra、ConTextTab）中特有的二维行/列注意力，构建了可复现的基准测试，在 A100、H100、B200 上比较 Torch SDPA、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM、SageAttention 的前向/反向吞吐。发现行与列注意力的最优后端不同，且随硬件、序列长度和头维度变化，FlashAttention 整体最佳但 cuDNN 在部分列注意力场景胜出，SageAttention 适合长行序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效注意力研究主要针对一维语言模型序列，而表格基础模型使用交替的行注意力（长序列）和列注意力（短序列）以及跨步内存布局，二维表格注意力尚未被系统研究，缺乏可复现的基准来指导后端选择。

Method: 论文聚焦表格基础模型的二维注意力机制。输入表格被展平为二维序列的潜在嵌入，模型使用交替的行注意力和列注意力：行注意力作用在较长的序列上（对每一列跨所有行），列注意力作用在较短的序列上（对每一行跨所有列）。由于表格数据的跨步内存布局，生成连续张量的开销较大。论文构建了可复现的基准测试框架，针对真实表格形状，评估多种注意力后端：Torch SDPA（efficient 与 cuDNN）、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM（仅推理）和 SageAttention（仅推理）。实验测量前向与反向吞吐量，覆盖 A100、H100、B200 三代 GPU。主要发现：行注意力和列注意力的最优后端不同，并随硬件世代、序列长度、头维度等模型细节变化。总体 FlashAttention 实现最佳，但在列注意力长序列下有时被 cuDNN 超越，交叉点取决于头维度；SageAttention 在行注意力且序列超过 16k 行时表现良好。该基准为未来表格原生注意力的改进提供了基础，代码开源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "forward/backward throughput", "value": "未提供具体数值，以定性比较呈现", "setting": "在 A100、H100、B200 上，对真实表格形状的行/列注意力，比较 Torch SDPA(efficient/cuDNN)、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM、SageAttention 的前向与反向吞吐"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建了可复现的表格注意力基准测试环境，覆盖真实表格形状的二维注意力。；系统比较了多种注意力后端：Torch SDPA（efficient 与 cuDNN）、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM 和 SageAttention。；在 A100、H100、B200 三代 GPU 上测量前向与反向吞吐量，揭示行注意力与列注意力的最优后端不同，且随硬件、序列长度、头维度变化而改变。；发现 FlashAttention 实现整体最佳，但在列注意力长序列下有时被 cuDNN 超越，存在取决于头维度的交叉点；SageAttention 在行注意力且序列超过 16k 行时表现良好。

Abstract: Tabular in-context learners such as TabPFN, Mitra, or ConTextTab rely on alternating row and column attention over 2D sequences of latent embeddings. These attention patterns differ markedly from the one-dimensional case in language models: row attention involves longer sequences while column attention operates on much shorter ones, and the strided memory layout of tabular data makes producing contiguous tensors costly. Moreover, the hidden dimensions used in current models are small compared to recent language models. Yet efficient attention has been studied mostly for one-dimensional sequences, leaving the two-dimensional tabular setting unexplored. To this end, we create a reproducible benchmarking setup and study the unique characteristics of tabular attention across several backends -- Torch SDPA (efficient and cuDNN), FlashAttention-2/3/4, and the inference-only backends vLLM and SageAttention -- measuring forward and backward throughput across realistic tabular shapes on three GPU generations (A100, H100, B200). We find that the optimal backend choice differs between column and row attention and varies across hardware as well as model specifics: While the FlashAttention implementations tailored for each GPU generation perform overall best, they are at times outperformed by CuDNN in the case of column attention at longer sequences with cross-over points depending on the head dimension. Among inference-only backends, SageAttention performs well for row attention and large sequences beyond 16\,k rows. Our reproducible benchmark lays the foundation for future improvements to table-native attention. The self-contained benchmarking and evaluation code is openly available at: this https URL

</details>


### [35] [Deterministic Regime Switching and Feasibility Inversion in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31250)
*Mahesh Reddy Pagadala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在参考 DTR 模拟器 simrd 上使用公开执行轨迹，报告了 Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization 的细粒度确定性不稳定性：LSTM 轨迹上预算差 0.10% 即导致快/慢区间切换、开销差最高 7.3x，慢区间由同一批存储被反复驱逐驱动（每存储驱逐次数 1.33→8.27，不同存储集合 Jaccard 0.999）；ResNet-32 轨迹出现可行性反转（0.101 可行、0.102-0.106 OOM、0.107 起可行），原因是完全钉住的递归重算前沿超出预算。消融指向 size-staleness 评分项。结果均为模拟器实验，生产复现为未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization (DTR) 作为内存受限 DNN 训练的在线驱逐策略，在微小显存预算变化下表现出确定性的执行不稳定性与可行性反转现象，其机制此前未被清晰刻画。

Method: 论文围绕 Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization (DTR) 这一内存受限 DNN 训练的在线驱逐策略展开，实验基于参考 DTR 模拟器 simrd 与公开执行轨迹。DTR 的运行机制为：在训练过程中跟踪张量存储的活跃性，当显存预算受限时按评分函数选择并驱逐张量存储，在被再次需要时通过重算（rematerialization）恢复；评分函数结合张量大小与 staleness（联合 size-staleness 评分项）。论文的管线为：输入 LSTM 与 ResNet-32 的公开执行轨迹 → 在 simrd 中设置不同内存预算比例（相对于 unconstrained peak memory）→ 运行 DTR 驱逐策略 → 记录执行时间开销、每存储驱逐次数、不同被驱逐存储集合及其 Jaccard 重叠、以及是否 OOM。核心发现包括：(1) LSTM 轨迹上，预算仅差 0.10% 即可使执行落入快/慢两个确定性区间，慢区间特征是同一批存储被反复驱逐，每存储驱逐次数由 1.33 增至 8.27，而不同被驱逐存储集合基本不变（5,233 vs 5,236，Jaccard 0.999）；(2) ResNet-32 轨迹上细粒度预算扫描揭示确定性可行性反转：ratio 0.101 可行、0.102-0.106 OOM、0.107 起再次可行，OOM 的直接原因是完全钉住的递归重算前沿在驱逐完所有可驱逐张量后仍超出预算；(3) 使用 DTR 作者自身变体做消融，指出联合 size-staleness 评分项与 LSTM 不稳定性相关。作者强调这是至少两种不同的预算敏感病理，并明确区分已验证结论与仍属假设的内容。所有结果均在参考模拟器上获得，生产运行时中的复现列为未来工作。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "快慢执行区间开销比", "value": "最高 7.3x", "setting": "LSTM trace，内存预算相差 0.10% unconstrained peak memory"}, {"metric": "每存储驱逐次数", "value": "1.33 → 8.27", "setting": "LSTM trace，慢区间 vs 快区间；不同被驱逐存储数 5,233 vs 5,236，Jaccard 0.999"}, {"metric": "可行性边界", "value": "ratio 0.101 可行；0.102-0.106 OOM；0.107 起再次可行", "setting": "ResNet-32 trace，细粒度预算扫描"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在参考 DTR 模拟器 simrd 上使用公开执行轨迹，发现细粒度确定性不稳定：内存预算相差 0.10% 即可使 LSTM 轨迹在快/慢执行区间间切换，开销差异最高达 7.3x；识别慢区间的直接驱动因素——同一批存储被广泛重复驱逐：每存储驱逐次数从 1.33 升至 8.27，而不同被驱逐存储集合几乎不变（5,233 vs 5,236，Jaccard 0.999）；在 ResNet-32 轨迹上发现确定性可行性反转：ratio 0.101 可行，0.102-0.106 不可行（OOM），0.107 起再次可行；将 OOM 直接原因定位为完全钉住的递归重算前沿在驱逐完所有可驱逐张量后仍超出预算；使用 DTR 作者自身变体进行消融，指出联合 size-staleness 评分项与 LSTM 不稳定性的关联；论证这是至少两种不同的预算敏感病理，而非单一机制，并区分已证明与仍属假设的内容

Abstract: We report fine-grained, deterministic instability in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization (DTR), an online eviction policy for memory-constrained DNN training, measured on the reference DTR simulator (simrd) using public execution traces. On an LSTM trace, memory budgets differing by 0.10% of unconstrained peak memory select fast and slow execution regimes whose overheads differ by as much as 7.3x; the slow regime is driven by broadly repeated re-eviction of the same storages (evictions per storage rise from 1.33 to 8.27 while the set of distinct evicted storages is essentially unchanged: 5,233 vs 5,236, with the two sets overlapping at Jaccard 0.999). On a ResNet-32 trace, a fine budget sweep reveals a deterministic feasibility inversion: the run is feasible at ratio 0.101, infeasible (OOM) across 0.102-0.106, and feasible again from 0.107. We trace the immediate cause of the OOM to a fully pinned recursive rematerialization frontier that exceeds the budget after every evictable tensor has been evicted. Ablations using the DTR authors' own variants implicate the joint size-staleness scoring term in the observed LSTM instability. We argue these are at least two distinct budget-sensitive pathologies rather than one mechanism, and we separate what is demonstrated from what remains hypothesised. All results concern the reference simulator; reproduction in a production runtime is future work. Code, instrumentation, and raw results accompany this preprint.

</details>


### [36] [Block Sparse Attention with Log-Linear Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31093)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PISA 提出金字塔 Top-K 选择策略实现 O(N log N) 复杂度的块稀疏注意力，通过粗到细层次结构逐层缩小候选键，并开发融合 Triton 内核，在语言建模任务上性能与基线相当且检索任务更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力随序列长度呈二次复杂度，块稀疏注意力虽可降低计算量，但传统块选择需对所有查询-块对打分，复杂度仍为二次，成为长上下文扩展的瓶颈。

Method: PISA 是一种块稀疏注意力机制。其核心流程为：首先通过池化操作构建键的粗到细层次结构，共 O(log N) 层，每一层的键由下一层更细粒度的键池化得到。给定查询，从最粗层开始进行 Top-K 选择：在每一层，对当前候选集使用 LogSumExp 打分，选出得分最高的 Top-K 个候选块，作为下一更细层的候选集，如此逐层向下，直到最细层确定最终参与注意力计算的键块集合。由于每层候选集有界，整体选择复杂度为 O(N log N)。在确定稀疏块后，仅对这些块执行注意力计算。训练和推理均使用定制的 Triton 内核，将层次路由与 LogSumExp 打分融合，避免显式构建完整的查询-键打分矩阵。论文在语言建模任务上评估该方法，在常识推理等基准上取得与基线相当的性能，并在检索任务上优于基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索任务性能", "value": "优于基线", "setting": "未披露具体数据集与数值，仅描述为在检索任务上取得更好结果"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出金字塔 Top-K 选择策略，通过从粗到细的键层次结构逐层缩小候选集，将块选择复杂度降至 O(N log N)。；在每一层使用 LogSumExp 打分对有限候选集进行评估，选择进入下一更细层的候选，避免全量查询-键打分矩阵。；通过池化构建 O(log N) 层键表示，实现整体 O(N log N) 复杂度。；开发面向训练和推理的硬件感知 Triton 内核，融合层次路由与 LogSumExp 打分，无需物化查询-键打分矩阵。

Abstract: Scaling language models to long contexts is limited by the quadratic cost of self-attention. Block sparse attention offers an efficient alternative, but selecting the retained blocks remains a bottleneck. Conventional block selection requires scoring all query-block pairs and therefore remains quadratic in sequence length. To address this issue, we propose PISA, a block-sparse attention mechanism that employs a pyramid Top-$K$ selection strategy. The main idea is to gradually narrow down the candidates across different levels, making it more efficient to find the most relevant keys. Specifically, we construct a coarse-to-fine hierarchy of keys and perform selection from the coarsest level. At each level, LogSumExp scoring is applied to a bounded candidate set to select candidates for the next finer level, continuing until the finest level is reached. Through pooling, we construct $O(\log N)$ levels of keys, yielding an overall complexity of $O(N\log N)$, where $N$ denotes the sequence length. We develop hardware-aware Triton kernels for both training and inference, fusing hierarchical routing and LogSumExp scoring without materializing the query-key score matrix. We further evaluate our method on language modeling tasks. Compared with the baseline, our method achieves comparable performance on benchmarks such as commonsense reasoning while delivering better results on retrieval tasks.

</details>

*Barak Gahtan,Ido Galil,Alex M. Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We introduce \emph{effective depth} ($\Deff$), a scalar diagnostic that treats the layer-wise residual stream of a transformer as a discrete-time process, measures how representation similarity decays with layer distance, and aggregates that profile into one number. Across sixteen decoder-only language models, $\Deff$ separates a structural consequence of residual accumulation from an empirical one: even maximally diverse orthogonal updates have the closed-form reference $F_L = 2L/(L+1)<2$, yet fifteen of sixteen default measurements lie below $F_L$ (Qwen3.5: 32--44\%, OLMo-2: 40--41\%, Pythia: 23--28\%). Matched references show that the gap is not caused by the persistent initial state or update-size imbalance, but is largely a calibrated signature of correlated residual updates rather than evidence that depth is unused. Symmetric position-0, token-normalisation, and top-PC controls show the regime is not reducible to BOS or top-PC artefacts: the lone above-reference default outlier joins the same regime, and all sixteen models are sub-reference after token-normalisation or top-1-PC removal. Intermediate checkpoints show that the regime is established early in OLMo-2 and stable through 5T tokens, while Pythia-1.4B follows a distinct decreasing trajectory. A controlled residual-carry intervention supports the mechanism, and $\Deff$ is best read as a \emph{global} accumulated-state diagnostic, not as a capability score or pruning method.

</details>


### [38] [Towards Understanding Momentum Acceleration in River-Valley Loss Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30957)
*Miao Lu,Zeyu Bian,Kaiyue Wen,Beining Wu,Siyu Chen,Tianhao Wang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 理论工作，分析动量在 LLM 预训练的 river-valley 损失景观中通过稳定大学习率来加速沿 river 的优化，指出动量的主要加速作用来自可容忍的更大学习率而非直接加速 river 跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 river-valley 损失景观下，动量如何影响大学习率稳定性及沿 river 的优化加速。

Method: 该论文为理论分析工作，未提出新的模型架构或系统实现。其核心分析对象是梯度下降与动量优化在 river-valley 损失景观中的动力学。根据摘要，river-valley 景观包含一个低损失流形（river）及两侧陡峭正交方向（mountains），长期优化进展主要取决于沿 river 的推进。论文建立理论分析，说明动量通过稳定大学习率（vanilla GD 无法容忍而不显著偏离 river）来加速优化；更大的学习率进而提升沿 river 的速度，从而获得更快的长期本质进展。另一个理论观察是，对于非常平坦且慢旋转的 river，动量本身并不直接贡献加速，主要加速来源是动量所允许的更大学习率。摘要未披露具体的网络结构、训练目标、数据流或推理路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 理论刻画动量通过稳定大学习率来加速沿 river 的优化，而非直接提升跟踪 river 的速度；揭示在 river 非常平坦且慢旋转时，动量本身不直接贡献加速，主要加速来自可容忍的更大学习率；将动量分析与 river-valley 景观及 warmup-stable-decay 学习率调度器关联

Abstract: The empirical success of pretraining large language models has inspired a deeper investigation into the underlying loss landscapes and the optimization dynamics. Recent empirical and theoretical study suggest that the training loss landscape often exhibits a "river-valley" structure, which features a low-loss manifold (river) flanked by sharp orthogonal directions with higher loss (mountains). In the long term, the optimization progress is determined primarily by the progress along the river. Within such a landscape, gradient descent with large learning rates can move faster along the river despite high apparent loss due to vertical oscillations, while a subsequent sharp decay in the learning rate suppresses these oscillations, revealing genuine optimization progress. This explains the recent success of warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning rate scheduler which, unlike cosine scheduling, keeps stable high learning rate and decays before producing intermediate checkpoints. Building on this foundation, in this work we take a step further and study the role of momentum within such a loss landscape. We establish theoretical analysis that characterizes how momentum accelerates optimization by stabilizing large learning rates that can not be tolerated by vanilla GD without deviating significantly from the river. The enabled large learning rate in-turn gives greater speed along the river and makes faster essential progress in the long run. Another intriguing observation from theory is that for a river-valley landscape with very flat and slow-spinning river, the momentum itself does not contribute directly to acceleration in terms of the speed of tracking the river, while the main acceleration comes from the admissible larger learning rate.

</details>


### [39] [CacheReforge: Bounded Recovery for Stale KV Caches under Evolving Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30884)
*Yuhang Cao,Yanzhou Mu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CacheReforge 把适配器演进下的过期 KV 缓存表示为逐层混合版本对象，利用逐层适配器锚点、敏感度、累积漂移与可执行重启边界，在直接复用、有界重算、完整后缀恢复之间选择，并用累积尾部影响区分依赖深度与功能重算视野。在 Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B 的持续 LoRA 更新与 16K 长上下文负载上，平均 KL 散度相对过期复用降低 92.4%，仅重算 5.44% 的层，缓存维护时间相对完整 prefill 降低 93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在适配器版本持续演进导致 KV 缓存过期时，用最小的重计算量恢复当前模型（当前适配器）的行为保真度，而不是完全重算受影响后缀。

Method: CacheReforge 面向适配器版本演化场景下的 KV 缓存复用。输入是当前序列上下文以及由较早适配器版本生成的、可能过期的 KV 缓存；系统把该缓存建模为逐层的混合版本对象，即不同层可能来自不同适配器版本。对每一层，系统维护适配器锚点（per-layer adapter anchors）、校准得到的敏感度（calibrated sensitivity）以及累积漂移（accumulated drift）三类信号。基于这些信号与可执行重启边界（executable restart boundaries），系统为每个层/位置选择三种处理路径之一：直接复用已有 KV、在有限范围内重算、或完整恢复受影响后缀。方法进一步将‘依赖深度’与‘功能重算视野’区分开：前者刻画状态间的影响传播，后者刻画真正需要重算才能恢复当前模型行为的范围；并用累积尾部影响（cumulative tail influence）判断有界恢复在何种条件下可保持当前模型输出保真。最终推理路径是按层执行选定的复用/重算策略，从而在保真度与缓存收益之间取得平衡。论文未披露训练目标、具体硬件、延迟与吞吐数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均 KL 散度", "value": "降低 92.4%", "setting": "相对朴素过期缓存复用（stale reuse），Qwen2.5-1.5B 与 Qwen2.5-7B，持续 LoRA 更新，16K HotpotQA 与 2WikiMQA 负载"}, {"metric": "重算层比例", "value": "5.44% 的层", "setting": "同上实验设置"}, {"metric": "缓存维护时间", "value": "降低 93.2%", "setting": "相对全新完整 prefill（fresh full prefill），同上实验设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将过期 KV 缓存表示为分层混合版本对象（layerwise mixed-version objects）；结合逐层适配器锚点、校准敏感度、累积漂移与可执行重启边界，在直接复用、有界重算、完整受影响后缀恢复之间选择；区分依赖深度与功能重算视野（functional recomputation horizon），并用累积尾部影响刻画有界恢复何时能保持当前模型行为

Abstract: Large language models rely on KV caching to reduce repeated prefill computation in long context and interactive applications. As lightweight adapters evolve, cached states reflect earlier versions, so stale reuse distorts current model outputs, while complete affected suffix recomputation restores fidelity at substantial cost. We seek minimal recomputation that recovers current adapter behavior. Existing systems track token, context, or stable adapter identity, but neither represent caches from earlier adapter versions nor distinguish update propagation from the recomputation required for behavioral recovery. To address these gaps, we introduce CacheReforge, which represents stale KV caches as layerwise mixed-version objects. It combines per-layer adapter anchors, calibrated sensitivity, accumulated drift, and executable restart boundaries to select direct reuse, bounded recomputation, or complete affected-suffix recovery. We distinguish dependency depth from the functional recomputation horizon and use cumulative tail influence to characterize when bounded recovery preserves current-model behavior. We evaluate CacheReforge on Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B with continual LoRA updates, including 16K HotpotQA and 2WikiMQA workloads. CacheReforge reduces mean KL divergence by 92.4% relative to stale reuse, while recomputing only 5.44% of layers and reducing cache-maintenance time by 93.2% relative to fresh full prefill. These results show that version-aware recovery preserves model fidelity and most KV caching gains.

</details>

*Hongren Chen,Jiayang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无校准、无需训练的低比特循环状态量化方法，利用可观测性格拉米安和归一化状态范围进行混合精度分配，在四比特下显著降低负对数似然超额，六比特时接近FP32基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合语言模型的循环状态量化通常使用八比特或更高，如何在无校准数据、无需旋转或训练的情况下，实现更低比特（如四比特）的循环状态量化并保持模型性能。

With per-token state quantization, a four-bit mean payload reduces excess negative log-likelihood by factors of 3.3--27.9 relative to the best of seven baselines across three hybrid models; metadata costs vary. At six bits, negative log-likelihood differs from the FP32-state baseline by less than 0.005 nats. Ablations separate gains from variable bit widths, decay weighting, and range normalization. Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates specialized capabilities into a single student, but existing practice typically hard-routes each prompt to a domain-matched teacher for the entire rollout. This dependence on prompt-level domain labels restricts using unlabeled training mixtures and leaves complementary signals from other teachers unused. We introduce MOPD-Router, a framework that routes supervision over the full teacher pool at each token, without domain labels or training a separate routing model. Its plug-in interface supports different metrics for selecting and weighting teacher-specific OPD signals. Within this interface, we propose ExpertAlign, which scores each teacher by whether its correction to the student at the current token expresses the specialization that teacher acquired during post-training, and compare it against two reference metrics built on teacher confidence (Entropy) and teacher-student discrepancy (Novelty). Experiments on unlabeled and domain-labeled training mixtures under strong-to-weak and same-size distillation scenarios show that ExpertAlign achieves the strongest overall performance in all four settings. On unlabeled data, it improves the overall score by 5.88 (+12.3%) points over Mean aggregation; on domain-labeled data, it outperforms standard MOPD by 3.95 (+7.8%) points without using available domain labels. These results demonstrate token-level routing can exploit cross-domain complementary supervision, and reduce exclusive reliance on prompt-level domain assignment. Code is available at: this https URL .

Abstract: Task performance need not determine which intervention mechanism an agent retains. We study causal retention: whether a frozen learned state answers a mechanism-probe map fixed independently of training, including action, context, direct target, value, and delay. For finite structural causal model classes, the optimal probe error is a Bayes decision risk. It vanishes exactly when every learning-interface fiber lies within one probe-answer fiber; any state obtained by post-processing that interface inherits the same lower bound. A posterior-coverage theorem characterizes budgeted retesting, while an exact edit decomposition shows that the shifted set is the unique support of an error-free target update. Causal Core implements these conditions through evidence-gated writing, readout filtering, temporal credit, hidden-context setup, and local diagnostic updates. Experiments cover finite causal systems, continuous simulators, an official TD-MPC2 world model, and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. A frozen Qwen last-layer probe reaches 0.958 balanced accuracy on source mechanisms but 0.583 on changed delays; the gated mechanism state reaches 1.000 and accepts only 0.056 of synchronized-readout candidates. In TD-MPC2, five target states per actuator recover effect-sign accuracy from 0.057 to 0.948 without degrading stable responses. Causal retention is therefore distinct from task sufficiency and source-domain decodability.

</details>

Abstract: Successful training of deep neural networks is highly dependent on the distribution of the initial weights. If the weights are too large, network training blows up; if they are too small, the model fails to learn features. Stable initialization is the optimal moderation between these two extremes. The conventional theory of random networks uses the Central Limit Theorem to control inter-neuron dependencies, which introduces distributional approximation error and coupling between layers. For networks with sine activations, we derive the uniform-phase initialization, which obviates distributional approximation and fully decouples the layers. Ours is the first work to use the sine function's periodic symmetry. Models trained with the uniform-phase initialization outperform the state of the art in neural representation tasks like image and audio fitting. We find that our untuned models are competitive with the best-tuned baselines from previous work and support $\mu$P width scaling.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained on expert demonstrations using cross-entropy (CE), even when the downstream objective is not to imitate the demonstrated solution but to produce any output accepted by a verifier. This mismatch is seen in verifiable domains with multiple correct solutions, such as mathematical reasoning and code generation, where training data may contain only one expert solution per problem. We show that minimizing cross-entropy can be misaligned with minimizing verifier risk; two policies can assign identical likelihood to the observed demonstrations while placing different probability mass on incorrect outputs. This is formalized through a learning-theoretic counterexample in which CE minimization selects a suboptimal policy. We identify that controlling the support of the learned policy can solve this problem by preventing probability mass from spreading to unsupported outputs. Since support size is non-differentiable and computationally intractable, we propose entropy-regularized cross-entropy (ER-CE), using token-level Shannon entropy as a tractable proxy. Finally, across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, we find that entropy-regularized training consistently improves verifier accuracy over standard cross-entropy. Our results identify a simple failure mode of imitation-based post-training in verifiable tasks and provide a practical objective that is better aligned with producing correct outputs.

</details>

Conclusion: 从理论上证明种群最优UDM预测器一般依赖时间，时间控制模型对观测上下文的信任程度。；在有限数据设置下，当加噪训练序列更接近其原始干净序列而非其他竞争序列时，经验最优预测器在扩散轨迹的大部分区域对时间几乎不敏感。；实证发现训练好的语言UDM在轨迹大部分区域时间敏感性有限，且时间无关预测器在许多数据集和训练目标下与时间条件模型竞争甚至超越。

Abstract: Uniform discrete diffusion models (UDMs) commonly use explicit time conditioning, but we find that it can often be unnecessary in practice. In this paper, we first show that the population-optimal UDM predictor generally depends on time: time controls how much the model should trust the observed context. We then show that this dependence can become negligible in finite-data settings relevant to language. When a corrupted training sequence remains much closer to its original clean sequence than to competing training sequences, the empirical-optimal predictor is nearly insensitive to time over most of the diffusion trajectory, where the guarantee weakens toward the high-noise endpoint. Empirically, trained language UDMs exhibit limited time sensitivity over most of the trajectory, while time-agnostic predictors remain competitive with, and often outperform, time-conditioned models across datasets and training objectives. These results challenge the use of explicit time conditioning in UDMs: although the population optimum depends on time, explicitly conditioning on it may often be unnecessary in practice.

</details>


### [46] [Offline Policy Evaluation as a decision support tool for designing Adaptive Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30273)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用方法论，利用固定A/B测试的历史数据，通过doubly robust离线策略评估结合ground-truth锚定的warm-start仿真，对不同上下文赌博机自适应策略与非自适应策略进行排序，从而判断何时值得部署自适应实验。在合成RCT、Hillstrom、Criteo Uplift、LaLonde等基准上验证，表明存在显著异质性时自适应策略优于固定分配，无异质性时收益很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何仅利用固定随机A/B测试的历史数据，判断哪些自适应（上下文赌博机）策略会优于原固定分配策略，以及何时值得部署自适应实验。

Method: 方法流程如下：输入为固定随机A/B测试（静态分配）下收集的日志数据，其中存在异质性处理效应。第一步，基于日志估计nuisance components（倾向得分、结果回归等）。第二步，使用doubly robust估计器对一组预先指定的策略进行评估与排序，这些策略包括自适应上下文赌博机策略与非自适应的固定分配策略。第三步，当可以获得ground truth时，将离线训练得到的策略部署到仿真器中，该仿真器复用完全相同的数据生成奖励概率，从而提供一个安全且以ground truth为锚定的环境，用于研究warm-start条件下从离线到在线的过渡。仿真使用具有已知异质性结构和oracle策略的合成随机对照试验进行验证。最后，在标准开放基准（Hillstrom、Criteo Uplift、LaLonde）上以policy-value与regret的视角进行复核。训练目标即策略价值最大化，推理时通过仿真或离线估计输出策略排名并据此决策是否部署自适应实验。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将离线策略评估（OPE）与受控warm-start仿真结合，形成从离线排序到上线过渡的安全评估流程。；使用doubly robust估计器在日志A/B测试数据上对预定义的自适应与非自适应策略组合进行排序。；在仿真中复用完全相同的真实奖励概率生成机制，构建ground-truth锚定的环境来研究warm-start下的offline-to-online转换。；提出以policy-value和regret视角重新解释Hillstrom、Criteo Uplift、LaLonde等开放基准的实践方法论。

Abstract: We investigate how historical data from fixed randomized experiments (A/B tests) can be used to inform the deployment of adaptive experiments based on contextual bandits. Given data collected under a static allocation, our goal is to assess which adaptive policies, if any, would have outperformed the original design and under what conditions. To this end, we combine off-policy evaluation (OPE) with a controlled warm-start simulation. From logged A/B test data exhibiting heterogeneous treatment effects, we estimate nuisance components and use doubly robust estimators to rank a portfolio of pre-specified adaptive and non-adaptive policies. When ground truth is available, we then deploy the same offline-trained policies in a simulator that reuses the exact data-generating reward probabilities, providing a safe, ground-truth-anchored environment to study the offline-to-online transition under warm starting. Using synthetic randomized controlled trials with known heterogeneity structures and an oracle policy, our results indicate that adaptive, context-aware policies improve upon fixed allocations when meaningful heterogeneity is present, while providing little benefit in its absence. We reinforce our findings on standard open benchmarks (Hillstrom, Criteo Uplift, and LaLonde), reinterpreted through a policy-value and regret perspective. Overall, our results provide a practical methodology for deciding when adaptive experimentation is worth deploying and how to select among competing adaptive policies using existing A/B test data.

</details>

*Nux Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统分析循环 Transformer 低比特 PTQ 的两类失败模式——反馈暴露（非残差循环入口量化误差被反复反馈）与校准盲区（一步 GPTQ 仅看第 0 步激活），并提出跨循环步累积 Hessian 的校准方法，在 9 个检查点上一致优于一步 GPTQ 与 RTN，并在 Huginn-3.5B 上恢复到 bf16 级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准训练后量化（PTQ）在权重跨循环步复用的循环 Transformer 上存在两类失效：量化误差在无恒等路径的循环入口被反馈放大（feedback exposure），以及一步 GPTQ 仅用第 0 步激活构建 Hessian，导致后续循环步使用的输入方向未被校准（calibration blindness）。

Method: 研究对象为循环 Transformer：权重在多个循环步间复用。论文分析指出，循环结构包含非残差的 loop-entry adapter 与残差核心（residual core）。标准 PTQ 中，逐通道 INT4 主要在非残差 loop-entry adapter 处失效，而量化残差核心损害较小，原因是量化层扰动循环状态且没有恒等路径，误差在后续步被反馈放大，即反馈暴露；该现象在线性滤波器与 Mamba 状态空间模型的受控实验中同样出现，说明其不限于 Transformer。另一失效模式为校准盲区：一步 GPTQ 基线仅从第 0 步激活构建 Hessian，使后续循环步才使用的输入方向权重近似为零。改进方案是在多个循环步上累积 GPTQ Hessian，使校准覆盖循环过程中出现的状态方向，从而在 9 个检查点上优于一步 GPTQ 与 RTN，并在 Huginn 上恢复 bf16 级精度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "主任务指标（劣于 RTN 的检查点数量）", "value": "一步 GPTQ 在 7 个循环架构的 9 个检查点中有 5 个劣于 RTN", "setting": "9 个检查点、7 种循环架构上的量化对比"}, {"metric": "主任务指标", "value": "跨步累积 Hessian 的 GPTQ 在全部 9 个检查点上均优于一步 GPTQ 与 RTN", "setting": "9 个检查点、7 种循环架构"}, {"metric": "精度", "value": "在 Huginn 上恢复到 bf16 级别精度", "setting": "Huginn-3.5B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并区分循环模型中 PTQ 的两类失效模式：反馈暴露（feedback exposure）与校准盲区（calibration blindness）；通过在线性滤波器与 Mamba 状态空间模型上的受控实验，证明反馈暴露并非 Transformer 特有；提出跨循环步累积 GPTQ Hessian 的校准方法，在 9 个检查点上均优于一步 GPTQ 与 RTN

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse weights across recurrence steps, making low-bit quantization especially attractive. We identify two distinct failure modes of standard post-training quantization. On Huginn-3.5B, per-channel INT4 fails primarily at the non-residual loop-entry adapter, while quantizing the residual core is much less damaging. We call this feedback exposure: a quantized layer perturbs the recurrent state without an identity path, and the resulting error is fed back at later steps. Controlled experiments on linear filters and Mamba state-space models show that feedback exposure also occurs outside transformers. Grouped INT4 reveals a separate failure, calibration blindness: our one-step GPTQ baseline builds its Hessian from step-0 activations, leaving input directions used later in the recurrence nearly unweighted. Across nine checkpoints from seven looped architectures, one-step GPTQ is worse than round-to-nearest (RTN) on the primary task metric for five checkpoints. Accumulating the GPTQ Hessian across recurrence steps outperforms both one-step GPTQ and RTN on all nine checkpoints and recovers bf16-level accuracy on Huginn. These results separate two questions for PTQ on looped models: where quantization error enters the recurrence, and which states calibration sees.

</details>


### [48] [RAZOR: Pruning Replaceable Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30465)
*Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAZOR 提出一种免训练的 MoE 专家剪枝方法，通过共识残差与精确单删除恒等式评估专家的功能可替代性。在四个模型、25% 与 50% 剪枝率下取得九任务最高宏平均，优于 REAP 2.12–5.59 分，并在所有匹配设置中降低反向 KL 散度。尽管无生产部署证据，但对 LLM 推理加速和模型压缩具有直接参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定专家剪枝预算下，如何选择要剪掉的 MoE 专家，以最大程度保留原模型的输出分布；现有方法基于专家使用频率或贡献幅度，但未考虑幸存计算能否替代被删专家的功能。

Method: RAZOR 是一种免训练、基于校准的 MoE 专家剪枝方法。输入为固定层输入下的校准 token 集以及原始 MoE 模型（路由器与专家池）。对于每个候选被删除专家，RAZOR 首先计算共识残差：专家输出相对于原始加权混合输出的偏差，以衡量该专家输出与当前混合共识的偏离程度。然后利用精确单删除恒等式，在固定层输入下量化删除该专家后混合权重的重新归一化以及路由器可能选择的填充专家对输出的影响，从而得到局部可替代性评分。该评分在校准 token 上聚合，形成全局分数。在给定剪枝预算下，依据聚合分数选择要剪除的专家，整个过程无需梯度或恢复训练。剪枝后得到压缩模型，可直接用于推理；论文在四个大模型上以 25% 和 50% 移除率验证任务保留与预测保真度，并分析生成多样性、格式和终止的变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "九任务宏平均（最高）", "value": "在全部 8 个设置中最高（GLM-4.7-Flash、Qwen3.6-35B-A3B、DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731、Hy3 各 25% 与 50% 专家移除）", "setting": "免训练剪枝对比，包含 REAP 等基线"}, {"metric": "任务得分提升", "value": "超过 REAP 2.12–5.59 分", "setting": "两个匹配 REAP 基线的骨干模型（未具体命名）"}, {"metric": "配对任务比较胜率", "value": "36/36 全部胜出", "setting": "匹配 REAP 基线的两个骨干模型"}, {"metric": "反向 KL 散度", "value": "在全部四个匹配设置中低于 REAP", "setting": "GLM-4.7-Flash 和 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 的 25% 与 50% 剪枝"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出功能性可替代性（functional replaceability）作为专家剪枝评分标准，而非使用频率或贡献幅度。；设计基于共识残差（consensus residuals）的评分：专家输出与原始加权混合的偏差。；利用精确的单删除恒等式（exact single-deletion identity）在固定层输入下考虑幸存者重归一化与路由器选择的填充专家，提供局部评分。；在校准 token 上聚合局部评分以进行预算剪枝，无需梯度或恢复训练。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models activate few experts per token but store the full expert pool. Expert pruning reduces this storage burden; at a fixed pruning budget, the goal is to preserve the original model's output distribution as closely as possible. Yet an expert's usage or contribution magnitude does not by itself determine the damage caused by its removal. What matters is whether the surviving computation can replace its function. We introduce RAZOR, a training-free expert pruning method that scores functional replaceability using consensus residuals: deviations of expert outputs from the original weighted mixture. An exact single-deletion identity at a fixed layer input accounts for survivor renormalization and router-selected refill, providing local scores aggregated over calibration tokens for budgeted pruning without gradients or recovery training. On GLM-4.7-Flash, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, and Hy3 at 25\% and 50\% expert removal, RAZOR achieves the highest nine-task macro average among the evaluated pruning methods in all eight settings. On the two backbones with matched REAP benchmark runs, it exceeds REAP by 2.12--5.59 points and wins all 36 paired task comparisons. It also lowers reverse KL relative to REAP in all four matched GLM-4.7-Flash and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model--budget settings. Analysis of responses generated by Qwen3.6-35B-A3B nevertheless reveals changes in diversity, formatting, and termination, underscoring that task retention and predictive fidelity do not ensure generation stability.

</details>


### [49] [HybridInfer: Thermal-Aware Reinforcement-Learning Tier Routing for On-Device, Edge, and Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30270)
*Simran Koul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HybridInfer提出一种热感知的强化学习路由策略，在端侧、边缘、云三级LLM间动态选择，使用手机热余量和查询复杂度作为状态，离线Q-learning训练。在真实Android设备上210条prompt实验表明，该路由在最低成本下质量显著优于两种手调启发式，并避免端侧持续推理的热崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-device LLM推理在旗舰Snapdragon设备上持续生成会因热约束导致GPU推理运行时崩溃或静默卡死，而现有多级路由器对热状态不敏感、缺乏真实移动硬件验证。

Method: HybridInfer是一个三级LLM推理路由系统：端侧部署Llama 3.2 3B，边缘部署Llama 3.1 8B并带检索，云端使用GPT-4o。路由器以手机热余量和查询复杂度估计为状态，通过离线训练的Q-learning策略选择推理层级。奖励函数综合质量、延迟、成本、热惩罚以及鼓励端侧执行的locality bonus。推理时根据当前状态选择层级并执行对应模型，以缓解端侧热压力并避免GPU运行时崩溃。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "响应质量优于两种手调启发式", "value": "配对Wilcoxon检验 p < 0.02", "setting": "真实Android基准测试，210条prompt"}, {"metric": "自适应条件中最低成本", "value": "最低成本（未披露具体数值）", "setting": "真实Android基准测试，210条prompt"}, {"metric": "端侧始终开启条件在可服务查询上匹配每查询质量但慢3-6倍且长查询失败", "value": "慢3-6倍，长查询失败", "setting": "真实Android基准测试，210条prompt"}]}

Conclusion: 将手机热余量与查询复杂度作为状态，离线训练Q-learning策略在三层（端侧/边缘/云）间选择推理层级；奖励函数同时权衡质量、延迟、成本、热惩罚与端侧执行的locality bonus，并指出locality bonus是热感知路由不全部卸载的必要条件；首次在真实移动硬件上使用on-device热余量选择不同能力LLM推理层级

Abstract: On-device inference with small language models keeps user data local, works offline, and incurs no per-query cost, so the on-device tier is preferred when it is adequate. It is thermally constrained, however, and I find the constraint is sharper than a slowdown: on a flagship Snapdragon device, sustained on-device generation destabilizes the GPU inference runtime, which crashes or silently wedges after a few consecutive queries. The failure lies in the current toolchain (OpenCL kernel compilation and long-prompt prefill on the mobile GPU), recurs even when the device is cool, and is worst for long generations. Multi-tier routers across on-device, edge, and cloud models can relieve this pressure, but existing routers are thermal-blind and typically evaluated in simulation or on non-mobile hardware. I present HybridInfer, a thermal-aware reinforcement-learning router for a three-tier hierarchy (on-device Llama 3.2 3B, edge Llama 3.1 8B with retrieval, cloud GPT-4o) that uses the phone's thermal headroom and a query-complexity estimate as state and selects a tier by an offline-trained Q-learning policy. Its reward trades quality against latency, cost, and a thermal penalty, plus a locality bonus crediting on-device execution. I show this bonus is a precondition for thermal-aware routing: without it the optimal policy offloads every query. On a real Android benchmark of 210 prompts, the learned router attains significantly higher quality than two hand-tuned heuristics (paired Wilcoxon, p < 0.02) at the lowest cost of any adaptive condition. Always-on-device conditions match per-query quality on servable queries but are three to six times slower and fail on long queries, so routing wins on latency, reliability, and coverage rather than quality. To my knowledge this is the first use of on-device thermal headroom to select among LLM inference tiers of differing capability on real hardware.

</details>

### [50] [Retail Product Search: A Practical Approach at Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31498)
*Darshan Sonagara,Qujiaheng Zhang,Ankit Singh,Alex Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Target 提出并部署了生产级电商混合搜索系统，结合词法搜索与向量搜索，采用加权交错融合多通道结果，并通过精度控制与性能优化保持低延迟。线上 A/B 显示 CTR +0.97%、订单转化 +0.98%、每访客需求 +1.10%，零结果搜索约减半，每日服务数百万用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关键词搜索难以处理自然语言/语义查询，而纯向量搜索可能丢失关键意图信号或返回低精度结果；需要在电商零售场景下平衡相关性、商业目标与低延迟，构建混合检索系统。

Method: 系统为混合搜索架构，输入包括用户查询与商品数据。首先进行数据预处理，构建词法与向量检索所需表示；随后训练 embedding 模型以支持向量搜索。在线检索时同时运行词法搜索与向量搜索两个通道，形成多通道候选结果。为提升最终结果集精度，引入精度控制机制。多通道结果通过融合策略合并，论文比较了多种融合策略并最终采用加权交错（weighted interleaving）。最后为满足生产部署的低延迟要求，实施了性能优化。线上 A/B 测试验证了系统相对词法-only 搜索在点击率、订单转化、每访客需求和零结果率上的提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "点击率（CTR）", "value": "+0.97%", "setting": "线上 A/B 测试，对比词法-only 搜索"}, {"metric": "订单转化率（order conversion）", "value": "+0.98%", "setting": "线上 A/B 测试，对比词法-only 搜索"}, {"metric": "每访客需求（demand per visitor）", "value": "+1.10%", "setting": "线上 A/B 测试，对比词法-only 搜索"}, {"metric": "零结果搜索（zero-result searches）", "value": "约减半", "setting": "线上 A/B 测试，对比词法-only 搜索"}]}

Conclusion: 结合词法搜索与向量搜索的混合检索系统设计，覆盖数据处理、embedding 训练、精度控制、多通道融合与低延迟优化的端到端方案；对比多种多通道结果融合策略后采用加权交错（weighted interleaving）进行结果融合；针对最终结果集的精度控制方法，缓解纯向量搜索低精度问题；为维持生产部署低延迟而采用的性能优化

Abstract: Search is one of the most important features in e-commerce, directly driving customer engagement and business growth. A good product search system must show both relevant and desirable results. However, retail search presents unique challenges. User intent can range from exact matches to open-ended discovery. Search systems must also balance multiple goals, such as relevance, revenue, and profit, while keeping response times low. Traditional keyword-based methods often fall short in handling natural language or semantic queries. Vector search helps alleviate these issues, but it can miss key intent signals or return low-precision results. In this paper, we present the design of a hybrid search system at Target that combines lexical and vector search. We describe our approach to data processing, embedding training, precision control for the final result set, multi-channel result fusion (where we compared fusion strategies and adopted weighted interleaving), and the performance optimizations used to maintain low latency for production deployment. Our method improves offline evaluation metrics, and in online A/B testing it raised click-through rate by 0.97%, order conversion by 0.98%, and demand per visitor by 1.10% over lexical-only search, while roughly halving zero-result searches. The resulting system is deployed at scale and serves millions of guests daily.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在SASRec中用物品共现图的拉普拉斯位置嵌入替代可学习位置嵌入，在四个公开序列推荐基准上多数排序指标提升，表明图结构信号可作为序数位置信号的有效替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐模型通常依赖可学习的位置嵌入来编码用户交互顺序，本文探究能否用从物品空间结构导出的图拉普拉斯位置嵌入替代这种序数信号。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders typically rely on learnable positional embeddings to encode the order of user interactions. In this work, we ask whether this ordinal signal can be replaced by a structural one derived from the item space. We propose to use Laplacian positional embeddings in SASRec: we build an item co-occurrence graph from training interactions, compute eigenvectors of its symmetric normalized Laplacian, and use them as frozen graph-derived positional embeddings. The backbone architecture and training objective remain unchanged. Experiments on four public sequential-recommendation benchmarks show that this simple replacement improves SASRec performance on most ranking metrics and remains competitive with strong positional and temporal encoding baselines. These findings indicate that item-item graph structure can be an effective substitute for standard ordinal positional embeddings in sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [52] [Recommendation World Models for Future-State Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30711)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Jianheng Tang,Zheng Lin,Jing Yang,Puzhen Wu,Zheng Xing,Victor C. M. Leung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UA-TWM，一个给已训练序列推荐 ranker 加装的未来状态控制接口：构造邻近 slate、预测其未来后果，并在效用约束下选择替代 slate。在 MovieLens-25M、KuaiRand-Pure 和 KuaiSim 上验证了跨 12 种 backbone 的 Recall@20、NDCG@20 和未来状态对齐提升，属于推荐系统长短期目标权衡方向的可读工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何让已训练的序列推荐排序器在决定展示哪个 slate 时，能够估计并控制该 slate 对用户未来状态和后续反馈的影响，而不仅仅优化即时 items 排序。

Method: UA-TWM 是附加在已训练序列推荐 ranker 之上的 utility-anchored world-model 接口。输入是当前用户状态、候选 slate 以及一个参考 slate。接口首先在参考 slate 附近构造邻近 slate 动作，然后通过世界模型估计这些动作的目标相关后果（即对未来反馈/状态的影响）。在 logged-replay 实例中，接口组合效用估计、目标增益估计以及经校准的失败风险预测来评估替代 slate；在 closed-loop 实例中，则使用一步状态-动作预测，并在观察到真实反馈后更新后续决策。选择阶段在效用约束下挑选替代 slate；若没有任何替代满足约束，则回退到参考 slate。最终输出一个选定的 slate 交给推荐系统展示。论文在 MovieLens-25M、KuaiRand-Pure 以及 KuaiSim 交互模拟器上评估该接口跨 12 种序列 backbone 的迁移效果和 repeated target-directed 交互表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Recall@20", "value": "在全部匹配的 logged backbone 上均有提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "MovieLens-25M 与 KuaiRand-Pure，12 种序列推荐 backbone"}, {"metric": "NDCG@20", "value": "在全部匹配的 logged backbone 上均有提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "MovieLens-25M 与 KuaiRand-Pure，12 种序列推荐 backbone"}, {"metric": "future-state alignment", "value": "在全部匹配的 logged backbone 上均有提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "MovieLens-25M 与 KuaiRand-Pure，12 种序列推荐 backbone"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 UA-TWM：一种 utility-anchored world-model 接口，在已训练 ranker 之上构造邻近 slate 动作并估计目标相关后果。；在 utility 约束下选择替代 slate，并保留参考 slate 作为无合格替代时的 fallback。；提供 logged-replay 实例：结合效用与目标增益估计及校准的失败风险预测进行离线选择。；提供 closed-loop 实例：使用一步状态-动作预测，并在观察到反馈后更新决策。；跨十二种序列推荐 backbone 的迁移评估，以及 KuaiSim 中的重复目标导向交互诊断。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation optimizes which items to rank, while each displayed slate also shapes subsequent feedback and user state. We study how a trained ranker can support decisions about these future consequences. We introduce UA-TWM, a utility-anchored world-model interface that constructs nearby slate actions, estimates their target-relevant consequences, and selects an alternative subject to utility constraints. The reference slate serves as a fallback when no alternative qualifies. A logged-replay instantiation combines utility and target-gain estimates with calibrated failure-risk prediction; a closed-loop instantiation uses one-step state-action prediction and updates its decisions after observed feedback. We evaluate transfer across twelve sequential backbones on MovieLens-25M and KuaiRand-Pure, and repeated target-directed interaction in KuaiSim. Attaching the interface improves Recall@20, NDCG@20, and future-state alignment for every matched logged backbone. Selection ablations reveal the utility and risk costs of aggressive target pursuit, while closed-loop diagnostics isolate the contribution of action-conditioned prediction. Local consequence modeling thus enables target-aware selection around a trained sequential ranker.

</details>


### [53] [Component Benchmark: Hierarchical Model Profiling for Large-scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30656)
*Dharak Kharod,Yuzhen Huang,Zhou Wang,Jackie Xu,Fuzail Khan,Jacky Zhou,Hao Yan,Lidong Zhao,Xizhou Feng,Yvonne Liu,Karthik Jayaraman,Praveen Ramachandran,Vishwa Karia,Yashasvi Makin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CB 是一个面向大规模推荐系统的分层模型剖析系统，支持子模块级性能刻画与树状交互可视化，并已应用于TB级模型、千卡GPU、日均100B样本的生产环境，帮助加速推荐模型性能分析与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准剖析工具仅提供端到端吞吐或算子级轨迹，无法归因到从业者关心的子模块层面，而大规模推荐模型结构异构、动态形状、算力强度低，导致性能分析困难。

Method: CB 是一个分层剖析系统，输入为大规模推荐模型的计算图或模块划分。系统以子模块为基本剖析单元，通过插件架构独立运行每个子模块的性能基准测试，收集时间、硬件利用等指标。剖析结果被组织成树状结构，经交互式可视化前端呈现。其数据流为：模型定义 → 子模块切分 → 插件执行子模块基准 → 指标收集 → 层级化树构建 → 可视化展示。训练与推理路径未详细说明，论文强调该工具用于辅助分析推荐模型在异构操作、动态形状下的性能特征。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Component Benchmark (CB)：以子模块为单位的层级化基准测试框架，独立刻画每个子模块性能。；树状交互式可视化：将性能数据组织为树结构，便于从业者定位子模块瓶颈。；插件式可扩展架构：支持灵活扩展以适配快速演进的推荐模型组合。

Abstract: Large-scale recommendation models pose distinct, under-explored profiling challenges. Most recommendation model architectures are structurally heterogeneous, intermixing memory-bandwidth-bound operations, small compute-bound dense layers, dynamic shapes from jagged categorical features, and low-arithmetic-intensity operations. Recommendation models evolve rapidly as modeling engineers experiment with compositions, often written without visibility into hardware execution characteristics. Standard profiling tools offer either end-to-end throughput or operator-level traces, but cannot attribute performance to the submodules that practitioners reason about. We present Component Benchmark (CB), a profiling system that independently characterizes each submodule performance in a hierarchical manner, providing a tree-structured, interactive visualization that brings performance clarity to ML practitioners. At its core, CB provides a simple yet extensible, submodule-based benchmarking framework with a plugin architecture that enables hierarchical performance analysis. These large-scale recommendation models are TB-scale, run on thousands of GPUs and ingest 100B examples per day. We demonstrate CB's effectiveness on common open sourced models and discuss how CB has been leveraged to accelerate modern recommendation model performance analysis and optimization.

</details>


### [54] [Embedding Subspace Partitioning for Dynamic Multi-Objective Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30601)
*Shaobo Zhang,Alice Leung,Yunxiang Ren,Ping Liu,Yuchin Juan,Qianqi Shen,Benjamin Le,Jianqiang Shen,Chengming Jiang,Ko-Cheng Wang,Vidya Krishnamurthy,Caleb Johnson,Fedor Borisyuk,Luke Simon,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LinkedIn提出ESP，将嵌入分解为任务感知子空间，用加权和替代单一内积，权重可在服务时动态调整；利用EOS分隔和注意力掩码保证子空间隔离，单一索引GPU kNN服务。在MS MARCO上覆盖帕累托前沿，并在LinkedIn 7000万+周活用户平台部署，实现动态检索重配置和关键业务指标显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双塔检索模型将所有目标坍缩到单一静态嵌入空间，训练后无法在服务时调整多目标权衡，且多目标联合优化会引入目标间干扰，导致次优权衡。

Method: ESP基于Transformer双塔架构。输入查询和文档分别编码，使用模型原生EOS token作为子空间分隔符，通过分段感知注意力掩码阻止不同子空间token之间的注意力交互，并重置位置编码以实现子空间隔离。每个子空间生成独立嵌入表示，检索时不再使用单一内积，而是计算查询与文档在各子空间相似度的加权和，权重可在服务时调整。训练时采用多目标损失但通过子空间隔离缓解干扰。服务阶段将所有子空间嵌入拼接为单一索引，利用GPU加速穷举kNN检索，无需为每个目标维护独立的近似最近邻索引。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Pareto frontier coverage", "value": "Consistently outperforms strong multi-task baselines across diverse operating points", "setting": "Open-source benchmark built from MS MARCO"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "key business metric lifts", "value": "significant key business metric lifts", "setting": "LinkedIn job matching platform (70M+ weekly users), dynamic retrieval reconfiguration"}]}

Conclusion: 将嵌入分解为任务感知子空间，用各子空间相似度的加权和替代单一内积，权重可在服务时动态调整。；针对Transformer双塔，利用原生EOS token作为分段分隔符，配合分段感知注意力掩码和位置编码重置，在单次前向中保证子空间隔离。；服务阶段通过GPU加速的穷举kNN在一个拼接索引上完成，无需为每个目标单独构建ANN索引。；在MS MARCO开源基准上验证单一ESP模型能覆盖广泛帕累托前沿，优于强多任务基线。；在LinkedIn生产环境实现动态检索重配置并带来关键业务指标显著提升。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems must optimize across competing objectives, balancing semantic relevance with business metrics such as engagement and revenue. While bi-encoders dominate large-scale retrieval due to their efficiency, they collapse these heterogeneous signals into a single static embedding space. This design creates a fundamental limitation: once trained, the retriever cannot adapt to shifting objective priorities at serving time without retraining. Moreover, joint optimization with multi-objective losses often induces interference between objectives, leading to suboptimal trade-offs. We propose Embedding Subspace Partitioning (ESP), a retrieval framework that decomposes the embedding into task-aware subspaces and replaces the single dot product with a weighted sum of per-subspace similarities, whose weights are tunable at serving time. For Transformer bi-encoders, ESP uses the model's native end-of-sequence token as a segment delimiter, with segment-aware attention masking and position encoding resets to guarantee subspace isolation in a single forward pass. Serving is performed via GPU-accelerated exhaustive kNN over one concatenated index, eliminating the need for per-objective Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) infrastructure required by multi-head approaches. We evaluate ESP on an open-source benchmark built from MS MARCO. A single ESP model traces a broad Pareto frontier, consistently outperforming strong multi-task baselines across diverse operating points. In LinkedIn's job matching platform (70M+ weekly users), ESP enabled dynamic retrieval reconfiguration and delivered significant key business metric lifts.

</details>


### [55] [Nearest but Not Dearest: Shared Curator-Feedback Infrastructure for Content-Only Search and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30568)
*Matt Sandler*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Feed.fm 的工业案例研究，展示了一个 B2B 音乐发现平台如何用共享的策展人反馈基础设施同时服务内容驱动的搜索与推荐。通过将策展人拒绝分解为声音失败与上下文失败，并分别路由到候选生成过滤器和嵌入重加权头，在 1,200 条生产策展人判断上使拒绝率相对下降 24.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内容驱动的音乐发现中，离线余弦相似度预测策展人判断效果差（38% 的余弦最近邻被拒绝），需要一种共享的反馈基础设施来同时服务搜索与推荐路径。

Method: 平台使用一个授权曲库、一个 LAION-CLAP 联合音频-文本嵌入空间、一个候选生成过滤器和一个排序头。搜索路径接收文本提示与氛围标签，推荐路径接收种子曲目与艺术家。两者共享同一嵌入空间进行候选生成。策展人判断作为主要反馈信号：拒绝被分解为声音失败（风格、节奏、情绪不匹配）与上下文失败（语言错误、节日内容、 devotional 内容、权利与歌词标志）。上下文失败被路由到候选生成阶段的约束过滤器，声音失败被路由到表示层的嵌入重加权头。这两个层位于搜索/推荐拆分之下，因此单个策展人循环可同时维护两个体验。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "拒绝率", "value": "从 38.17% 降至 28.83%（-24.5% 相对下降，McNemar chi-squared = 22.4，p = 2.2e-6）", "setting": "1,200 条策展人判断，来自两轮生产部署（相隔一个月），未设盲且为复合干预"}, {"metric": "会计分解归因的下降", "value": "4.08 个百分点归因于过滤合格类别，5.25 个百分点归因于其余类别", "setting": "生产会计上限，非因果估计"}]}

Conclusion: 将策展人拒绝分解为声音失败（55%）与上下文失败（37%）的声-上下文字段分解；将失败模式路由到共享层：上下文失败进入候选生成阶段的约束过滤器，声音失败进入表示层的嵌入重加权头；共享策展人反馈循环，同时维护搜索与推荐体验

Abstract: A deployed B2B music-discovery platform serves both query-driven search (text prompts, vibe tags) and seed-driven recommendation (seed-track and artist stations) over one licensed catalog, one LAION-CLAP joint audio-text embedding space, one candidate-generation filter, and one ranking head -- and neither path consumes end-listener behavioral signal. In this content-only regime, curator judgment is the principal feedback signal available, and offline cosine similarity predicts it poorly: 38% of cosine-nearest neighbors are rejected by curators. The rejections reveal a clean partition: a majority (55%) are sound failures the encoder could address (style, tempo, mood mismatch), and a substantial minority (37%) are context failures orthogonal to the waveform (wrong language, holiday content, devotional content, rights and lyric flags). We deploy this sound-vs-context decomposition as feedback infrastructure, routing each failure mode to the layer that can absorb it: context failures to a constraint filter at candidate generation, sound failures to an embedding reweighting head at the representation layer -- both below the search/recommendation split, so a single curator loop maintains both experiences. On 1,200 curator judgments collected over two production rounds one month apart, the combined intervention reduces rejection rate from 38.17% to 28.83% (-24.5% relative, McNemar chi-squared = 22.4, p = 2.2e-6). An accounting decomposition attributes 4.08 pp of the drop to filter-eligible categories and 5.25 pp to the rest; the deployment was unblinded and compound, so this is a production accounting bound, not a causal estimate. We present this as an industrial case study rather than a validated general method, and close with lessons for content-only discovery: the failure partition is orthogonal to the paradigm partition, and corrections land in layers shared by both paradigms.

</details>


### [56] [KuaFu: Compressing Long User Behavior into Understanding at Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31045)
*Jiahao Hui,Lin Zhu,Yishen Hu,Jingdong Shu,Zetai Jiang,Xining Ran,Ben Tan,Yeshou Cai,Gong Chen,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 腾讯提出 KuaFu 统一用户行为压缩层，以单个行为条目为最小单元，双轴压缩至 2-4 tokens/宽 128-256，配合四阶段保真训练与分层中间评估，解决十亿用户每周刷新的吞吐瓶颈。四个生产任务五项指标不降，每 GPU 吞吐提升 37%-350%，节省 190 张 GPU，线上运行十个月并提升整体 GMV 1.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业用户理解通常是任务特定的：每个任务从全量历史中抽取相关子序列并训练专用模型。生产中有两个瓶颈：单任务序列即使过滤后仍极长（数百条内容、序列化为 prompt 后数万 token），且十亿用户每周刷新造成固定 GPU 预算下的硬吞吐下限。必须压缩，但截断或粗粒度压缩会悄然扭曲画像，产生编造、遗漏、日期错配、逻辑断裂四类幻觉，且压缩表示本身缺乏评估，只能表现为下游指标弥散式退化。

Method: KuaFu 是一个统一的行为压缩层，输入是用户的原始行为序列，最小处理单元为单个行为条目。每个行为条目经双轴 projector 压缩：token 轴约压缩 10 倍，宽度轴约压缩 20 倍，最终每个条目表示为 2-4 个宽度 128-256 的 token，单条目缓存由 10 KB 降至 0.5 KB。该压缩表示替代原始超长 prompt 文本进入下游的单任务生产模型，使单任务序列即使包含数百条内容、序列化为数万 token 也不再需要以原始形式输入。训练采用面向保真度的四阶段流程，目标是使压缩表示保留足够信息以支撑多个下游 profiling 任务，同时减少编造、遗漏、日期错配和逻辑断裂四类幻觉。为检测压缩表示本身的质量，方法使用分层中间评估，在压缩表示层面而非仅在下游指标上评估保真度。推理时，压缩层以十亿用户每周约 10 万 QPM 的刷新节奏运行，在固定 GPU 预算下通过 10 倍 token 压缩与 20 倍宽度压缩降低表示规模，从而提升每 GPU 吞吐并减少 GPU 需求。下游使用同一压缩表示服务四个生产 profiling 任务，线上广告与推荐平台运行十个月。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "EM", "value": "最高 +17.7 EM", "setting": "公开基准 MRQA 域外（out-of-domain），与先前压缩器在相同压缩比下比较"}, {"metric": "综合得分", "value": "4B 模型超过 8B 对照 1.90 分", "setting": "RecBench 推荐基准，KuaFu 压缩表示下 4B 模型 vs 8B 对照"}, {"metric": "五项 headline 指标", "value": "在全部五项上匹配或超过未压缩的单任务生产模型", "setting": "四个生产 profiling 任务的离线评估"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "GMV", "value": "+1.37%", "setting": "腾讯广告与推荐平台整体，已线上运行十个月"}, {"metric": "每 GPU 吞吐", "value": "+37% 至 +350%", "setting": "生产 profiling 任务，固定 GPU 预算下"}, {"metric": "GPU 节省", "value": "190 张 GPU", "setting": "生产部署"}]}

Conclusion: 以单个行为条目为最小压缩单元的统一点式行为压缩层；双轴 projector：沿 token 轴约 10 倍、沿宽度轴约 20 倍压缩，每个条目压缩为 2-4 个宽度 128-256 的 token，单条目缓存从 10 KB 降至 0.5 KB；面向保真度的四阶段训练流程；分层中间评估，用于在压缩表示层面检测四类幻觉而非只依赖下游指标；一个压缩层服务四个生产 profiling 任务，并在 RecBench 上使 4B 模型超过 8B 对照 1.90 分

Abstract: Conversational agents, generative recommenders, and personalized advertising all rest on one capability: understanding each user from raw behavior. Prevailing industrial practice is task-specific: for each task, a relevant subsequence is extracted from the full history and a dedicated model trained on it. In production it hits two bottlenecks. First, even after filtering, a single-task sequence stays extremely long: content-interest summarization reads several hundred items per user, tens of thousands of tokens once serialized as prompt text. Second, profiles are refreshed routinely: a billion users weekly, roughly 100K QPM in aggregate, which under a fixed GPU budget sets a hard throughput floor. Compression is therefore mandatory, yet truncation or coarse compression can silently distort the profile, introducing four hallucination types (fabrication, omission, date misattribution, broken logic) that, with no way to evaluate the compressed representation itself, surface only as diffuse degradation in downstream metrics. We present KuaFu, a unified behavior-compression layer whose minimal unit is one behavior item. A two-axis projector compresses each item into 2-4 tokens of width 128-256 (about 10x along the token axis, 20x along width; per-item cache 10 KB to 0.5 KB), with fidelity-oriented four-stage training and layered intermediate evaluation. Across four production profiling tasks it matches or exceeds uncompressed single-task production models on all five headline metrics, raises per-GPU throughput by 37%-350%, and saves 190 GPUs. On public benchmarks it nearly always beats prior compressors at the same compression ratio (up to +17.7 EM on out-of-domain MRQA); on RecBench, a 4B model surpasses its 8B counterpart by 1.90 points. KuaFu has run on the Tencent advertising and recommendation platform for ten months, lifting overall GMV by 1.37%.

</details>


### [57] [RecToolBench: Benchmarking Recommendation-Specific Tool Orchestration under Fuzzy User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30717)
*Xiao Chen,Yicheng Zhao,Yingying Wu,Zhendong Chu,Changyi Ma,Qingsong Wen,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecToolBench是一个基于MCP的推荐专用工具编排基准，包含1200多个可执行任务、13个服务器和32个工具，用于评估模糊意图下的智能体推荐能力，发现语法有效工具调用不等于推荐成功，语义参数接地与多步证据整合是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐智能体基准通常假设用户意图明确、工具环境简化或仅单次函数调用，无法反映真实模糊用户意图下的工具编排能力。

Method: RecToolBench的构造与评估流程包含三个主要阶段。首先，通过可扩展的合成—模糊化—评判流水线生成任务：以推荐场景为起点合成结构化、可执行的任务，再将用户意图模糊化，最后以评判机制筛选有效样本。其次，构建基于Model Context Protocol的执行环境，包含13个MCP服务器和32个工具，覆盖3个推荐领域，并支持单工具调用、并行工具调用、顺序工具链与混合工具编排四类任务结构，任务总数超过1200个。评估阶段同时使用规则化执行检查验证工具调用是否可执行，以及基于rubric的LLM评估对智能体轨迹进行语义与推荐质量判断。实验中，代表性LLM在语法有效的工具调用之外仍难以完成语义参数接地、多步证据整合和最终推荐生成，因此该基准揭示工具编排复杂度增加时推荐智能体的主要瓶颈。摘要未披露模型训练目标、参数规模、训练数据、硬件或推理延迟等信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于Model Context Protocol的推荐专用工具编排基准RecToolBench；覆盖3个推荐领域、13个MCP服务器、32个工具、1200多个可执行任务；覆盖单工具调用、并行工具调用、顺序工具链和混合工具编排四类复杂度；设计合成—模糊化—评判的可扩展流程来生成可执行且意图模糊的推荐任务；结合规则执行检查与基于rubric的LLM评估来评价智能体轨迹

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic recommender systems are shifting recommender systems from passive filtering engines to instruction-following agents that use external tools to resolve user intent. However, existing benchmarks often assume explicit user intent, simplified tool environments, or isolated function calls, leaving realistic tool orchestration for recommendation underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose RecToolBench, a Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based benchmark for evaluating tool-using recommender agents under fuzzy user instructions. RecToolBench contains more than 1,200 executable tasks across three recommendation domains, 13 MCP servers, and 32 tools, spanning single-tool calls, parallel tool calls, sequential tool chains, and hybrid tool orchestration. We construct RecToolBench with a scalable synthesize--fuzzify--judge pipeline that generates executable fuzzy recommendation tasks, and evaluates agent trajectories using rule-based execution checks and rubric-based LLM evaluation. Experiments on representative LLMs show that syntactically valid tool calls do not guarantee successful recommendations. Models struggle with semantic parameter grounding, multi-step evidence integration, and grounded final recommendations, especially as orchestration complexity increases. Our results identify tool orchestration under fuzzy user intent as a major bottleneck for agentic recommender systems. Our data and code are available at this https URL .

</details>
