### [58] [T-RoPE: Time-Aware Rotary Position Embedding for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30576)
*Yang Liu,Noel Loo,Ali Khanafer,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni,Zhong Wu,Linjun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T-RoPE提出时间感知旋转位置编码，将交互索引替换为时间戳角度并引入多尺度频率与非平稳键旋转，在五个公开基准和超6B交互的工业数据集上全面超越基线，并在Shop应用A/B测试中提升转化率和订单量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RoPE使用交互索引编码位置，只记录事件顺序，无法捕捉真实时间间隔、跨尺度行为周期和日历相位，导致序列生成式推荐模型的时间感知能力不足。

Method: T-RoPE将标准RoPE的索引位置替换为基于时间戳的旋转角度，使注意力能够感知真实时间间隔。具体地，它包含可学习的时间系数，用于调整不同时间戳对旋转频率的贡献；多尺度频率库提供不同时间粒度的频率分量，使模型能捕捉从短时行为到长周期节律的模式；移位查询对齐对查询向量进行时间对齐，增强相对时间推理；非平稳关键旋转在键向量上施加随时间变化的旋转，从而打破时间平移不变性，区分季节性上下文。整个方法保持RoPE的接口形式，可直接替换现有Transformer中的位置编码。作者证明了标准RoPE即使基于时间戳仍保持时间平移不变性，而T-RoPE打破该不变性。前向与反向传播算法保证额外计算成本与序列长度和头维度线性相关，适合大规模生成式推荐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "HR@10", "value": "78-130% 提升", "setting": "稀疏PixelRec数据集，对比最强基线"}, {"metric": "多项指标", "value": "8-12% 提升", "setting": "Amazon Books数据集"}, {"metric": "全部指标", "value": "13-82% 提升", "setting": "工业级电商数据集，超过6B交互，对比HSTU + Time RAB骨干网络"}, {"metric": "NDCG@50", "value": "+56%", "setting": "消融实验，归因于多尺度频率"}, {"metric": "NDCG@50", "value": "+4%", "setting": "消融实验，归因于关键非平稳旋转"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "转化率", "value": "+0.33%", "setting": "Shop应用在线A/B测试"}, {"metric": "订单量", "value": "+0.63%", "setting": "Shop应用在线A/B测试"}]}

Conclusion: 提出T-RoPE，用时间戳角度代替仅索引旋转，并引入可学习时间系数；多尺度频率库，使模型捕捉不同时间粒度的行为周期；移位查询对齐与关键非平稳旋转，打破RoPE的时间平移不变性；证明标准RoPE即使使用时间戳仍具有时间平移不变性，无法区分季节性上下文，而T-RoPE在保留RoPE接口的同时打破该不变性；给出前向与反向算法，额外计算成本随序列长度和头维度线性增长

Abstract: Large-scale recommenders increasingly adopt the sequential generative recipe behind large language models, bringing the Transformer into recommendation along with design choices made for text, including Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE). In language models, RoPE encodes token indices for relative position reasoning, but in recommendation, an interaction index records only event order, saying nothing about elapsed time, behavioral cycles across scales, or calendar phase. We revisit this choice and propose T-RoPE, a time-aware RoPE for sequential generative recommendation that replaces index-only rotation with timestamp-based angles, learnable temporal coefficients, multiscale frequency banks, shifted query alignment, and non-stationary key rotation. We prove that standard RoPE, even on timestamps, remains time-translation invariant and cannot distinguish seasonal contexts, and that T-RoPE breaks this invariance while preserving the RoPE interface. Across five public benchmarks, T-RoPE achieves the best result on every metric on every dataset, improving over the strongest baseline by 78--130\% in HR@10 on the sparse PixelRec data and 8--12\% across metrics on Amazon Books. On an industrial-scale e-commerce dataset with more than 6B interactions, it improves every metric over the HSTU + Time RAB backbone by 13--82\%, with ablations attributing the largest gains to multiscale frequencies ($+56\%$ NDCG@50) and non-stationary keys ($+4\%$). An online A/B test in the Shop app yields positive lifts in conversion rate ($+0.33\%$) and order count ($+0.63\%$). We also provide forward and backward algorithms whose added cost is linear in sequence length and head dimension, keeping time-aware RoPE practical for large generative recommenders.

</details>

*Quang Nguyen,Hieu Nguyen,Hien Hoang,Toan Pham,Cong Tran,Nam Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepEdu-v1提出SCALE框架，面向越南教育场景的自托管agentic LLM辅导系统。通过长上下文推理引擎将token选择从每子块粒度摊销到每簇粒度，减少检索调用并降低prefill延迟；同时使用免微调的自改进agentic层从交互中积累验证playbook。部署配置下实现接近x2 TTFT加速，agentic准确率从70.0%提升至79.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在越南教育场景下，云端助手存在数据主权和本地课程知识不足/幻觉问题，而自托管开源模型又面临长辅导上下文的KV cache、prefill延迟和OOM瓶颈，以及区域知识持续幻觉的问题。

Method: DeepEdu-v1基于SCALE（Self-improving Context-Aware Learning Engine）框架，面向越南教育AI辅导。系统输入为长辅导上下文与检索内容，在自托管消费级GPU上运行。架构包含两个主要模块：一是长上下文推理引擎，其核心是将token选择从per-sub-chunk粒度摊销到per-cluster粒度，从而在长上下文检索时减少检索调用次数并降低prefill延迟（TTFT），同时保持或提升任务准确率；二是自改进agentic层，不进行模型微调，而是持续从过去的交互中整理出经过验证的playbook，用于指导后续agent行为，随着可信本地知识的积累逐步减少对主导语言先验的依赖。部署时结合标准vLLM serving，在部署配置下实现接近x2的TTFT加速和agentic准确率从70.0%到79.5%的提升。论文未披露具体模型参数规模、训练数据、硬件型号、吞吐或成本等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "TTFT速度提升", "value": "接近x2", "setting": "部署配置下，相比标准vLLM serving"}, {"metric": "agentic准确率", "value": "70.0% -> 79.5%", "setting": "复杂任务，部署配置"}]}

Conclusion: 长上下文推理引擎将token选择从每子块粒度摊销到每簇粒度，减少检索调用并降低prefill/TTFT。；自改进agentic层：持续从历史交互中整理经过验证的playbook，而不是进行微调，以逐步减少对主导语言先验的依赖并积累可信本地知识。

Abstract: AI tutoring could markedly improve learning outcomes for students in developing regions such as Vietnam, yet the two obvious paths both fall short. Cloud assistants such as ChatGPT route sensitive student data to foreign servers---violating data-sovereignty laws such as Vietnam's Decree 53---and, pre-trained on Western-centric corpora, are not organized around the national textbook curriculum, so their knowledge of local content is unsystematic and frequently hallucinated. Self-hosting an open model keeps data on-premise but hits a two-fold wall: post-training quantization (AWQ, GPTQ) tames the static weight footprint, yet the dynamic KV cache and prefill latency of long tutoring contexts still cause out-of-memory failures and slow responses on consumer GPUs, while the model keeps hallucinating on region-specific material. We present DeepEdu-v1, an AI-tutoring system for Vietnamese education built on SCALE (Self-improving Context-Aware Learning Engine), a framework with two innovations. First, a long-context inference engine amortizes token selection from per-sub-chunk to per-cluster granularity; on long-context retrieval it issues x7.7 fewer retrieval calls than a state-of-the-art selective-attention baseline, cutting prefill latency (TTFT) by roughly 35% while matching or improving task accuracy. Second, a self-improving agentic layer continuously curates a verified playbook from past interactions instead of fine-tuning, a design intended to progressively reduce reliance on dominant-language priors as trustworthy local knowledge accumulates. In its deployed configuration, DeepEdu achieves a nearly x2 TTFT speedup over standard vLLM serving and lifts agentic accuracy from 70.0% to 79.5% on complex tasks, with the strongest per-track gains across financial-reasoning and interactive-agent benchmarks.

</details>

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "完整竞赛运行结果", "value": "paper reports full competitions across models for Chess, Poker, and Werewolf", "setting": "Kaggle Game Arena 平台上的三个试点环境"}]}

Conclusion: 提出 Kaggle Game Arena，一个开放、可扩展的 LLM 竞技性评测平台；设计三个覆盖完全信息、不完全信息和多人博弈的试点环境：国际象棋、扑克、狼人杀；构建大规模、基于真值（ground-truth）的自动评测与对战基础设施，支持持续竞赛与可复现评估；通过头对头对战使评测难度随模型进化自然提升，避免性能饱和

Abstract: We introduce Kaggle Game Arena, an open and ever-expanding platform to evaluate large language models (LLMs) through competitive games. Different from static benchmarks, game arena enables models to play head-to-head matchups in structured environments where the gameplay strength naturally increases as models evolve, preventing performance saturation. This technical report details the infrastructure behind Game Arena and describes the three pilot game environments: Chess, Poker, and Werewolf. These environments span perfect information, imperfect information, and multiplayer game settings, enabling a systematic study of models' strategic planning, adaptation, and robustness under uncertainty. For each game, we provide a detailed description of the environment, evaluation metrics, and results from running full competitions across models. Through robust infrastructure and large-scale ground-truth based evaluation, Game Arena ensures reproducibility, transparency and generalizability to new games and variants over time.

</details>


### [61] [Programs-of-Layers in LLMs through the Lens of Cortical Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31360)
*Justus Westerhoff,Stephan Olbrich,Hatem Oraby,Matthew Evan Larkum,Felix Alexander Gers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在 5 个模型上重建并复现了 LLM 层程序（PoLar）的动态层路由：跳过、重复连续层块可优于标准前向传播，难题需更多重复；但学习路由器的 top-1 预测塌缩回标准前向传播，top-k 程序集合才有增益。还发现少量通用程序即可解决多数问题，纠错程序对单次编辑高度脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理固定深度、固定顺序的前向传播无法根据输入难度调整计算量，而大脑通过丘脑-皮层路由机制灵活分配计算资源；论文检验将 Transformer 层视为函数库、对输入动态路由自适应层块序列（跳过或重复连续层块）的可行性、可复现性与鲁棒性。

Method: 论文将 Transformer 的每一层视为可调用的函数，形成层函数库；推理时不再固定按顺序执行全部层，而是为每个输入动态生成一个层程序（program-of-layers）：程序由若干连续层块及其执行模式组成，模式包括跳过（skip）连续层块、重复（repeat）连续层块，或将两者组合。论文重建了 PoLar 的诊断式 MCTS 搜索流程，以更细粒度探索这些层程序空间，并在 5 个模型上评估。搜索得到的程序在难度不同的输入上表现不同：简单问题用较短程序即可，难题需要更多层重复。论文还分析了学习到的单次推理路由器：它输出程序分布，但 top-1 预测塌缩为标准前向传播，而 top-k 程序集合联合执行可获得准确率提升。鲁棒性分析对程序内部做单次编辑，发现纠错程序高度脆弱。整体推理路径为：输入经过路由器或 MCTS 诊断选择层程序，再按程序指令调用相应层块完成前向计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "跳过层优于标准前向传播；重复层优于跳过；结合两者优于单独使用（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "5 个模型上的 MCTS 诊断实验"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "学习路由器的 top-k 预测程序集合显示出真实准确率增益（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "单次推理路由器评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以比原论文更详细的 MCTS 诊断方法重建 PoLar 的层程序搜索，并在 5 个模型上复现与检验其结论；发现原论文学习到的单次推理路由器 top-1 预测塌缩回标准前向传播，但 top-k 预测程序集合存在真实准确率增益，揭示复现矛盾；发现少量通用程序即可解决多数问题，且纠错程序高度脆弱：在程序内部做单次编辑通常破坏纠错效果；将 PoLar 的层间路由与大脑丘脑-皮层协调机制相类比，提供跨学科解释视角；公开代码以支持可复现研究

Abstract: Inference in LLMs is conventionally a fixed-depth, fixed-order forward pass through every layer, regardless of how difficult the input is. The human brain does not work this way: using the thalamus as a central hub, it routes information flexibly to all regions of the cortex according to demand. Li et al. (2026) recently showed, with a system they call program-of-layers (PoLar), that transformers can be given an analogous flexibility if their layers are treated as a library of functions rather than a fixed sequence. Performance improves over the standard forward pass when each input is dynamically routed through an adaptive sequence of skipped or repeated contiguous layer blocks. We reconstructed PoLar's diagnostic MCTS in more detail than the original paper and applied it across 5 models. We reproduced several of PoLar's findings: skipping outperformed the standard pass, repeating outperformed skipping, and combining both outperformed either alone. Shorter programs sufficed for easier questions, while harder questions required more layer repeats. However, we failed to replicate the main claim regarding their learned router for single-shot inference: its top-ranked prediction consistently collapsed back to the standard pass, even though its top-k predicted programs, taken together, did show a real accuracy gain. Beyond reproduction, we find that a small number of generic programs are enough to solve most of the questions. We also provide a much deeper analysis of these programs' structure and robustness: for example, we found that programs that correct errors are highly brittle: undoing even a single edit inside a program typically breaks the correction. Connecting this to the brain's routing mechanisms, PoLar mirrors principles of thalamo-cortical coordination between cortical-area-like transformer layers. We publicly release the code at this https URL

</details>


### [62] [Mutable Transcripts: Mitigating Context Pollution through Editable Conversation State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31354)
*Dan Barry,Andrew Hines*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出可变对话记录范式，允许用户通过自然语言编辑历史轮次以减少上下文污染。用户研究（n=17）显示在清晰度、信心和易用性上显著优于标准聊天，并减少重启对话意愿。属于LLM对话系统交互与状态管理的探索性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM聊天系统将对话历史视为不可变序列，导致用户意图变化时出现过时或无关信息残留的上下文污染问题，影响后续回复质量。

Method: 系统在标准聊天界面中集成转录级修订功能。用户通过自然语言编辑请求（edit requests）修改之前对话轮次，系统解析这些请求并更新对话历史表示，使后续生成基于更新后的状态而非原始追加序列。原型支持将修订后的转录作为模型工作上下文，从而减少过时或无关信息对后续回复的影响。论文通过受控用户研究（n=17）评估可行性，并分析代表性交互场景的转录变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出可变对话记录（mutable transcripts）交互范式，将对话历史从被动记录转变为可编辑的会话状态表示；允许用户通过自然语言编辑请求修订之前轮次，而非仅追加新内容；在标准聊天界面中集成转录级修订功能并实现工作原型

Abstract: Contemporary large language model (LLM) chat systems treat conversation history as an immutable sequence of turns that defines the model's working context. However, user intent in real interactions is not static: it evolves through correction, refinement, and shifting constraints. This mismatch between dynamic intent and static transcripts can result in context pollution, where outdated or irrelevant information persists and continues to influence subsequent responses. We introduce mutable transcripts, a new interaction paradigm that enables users to revise prior turns through natural language edit requests, allowing the conversation history itself to be updated rather than appended. This reframes the transcript from a passive record into an editable representation of conversational state. We present a working prototype that integrates transcript-level revision into a standard chat interface and evaluate its feasibility through a controlled user study (n=17) and an illustrative transcript analysis of representative interaction scenarios. Participants significantly preferred mutable transcripts over standard chat across measures of clarity, confidence, and ease of use, with reduced intent to restart conversations. Transcript analysis of representative user study conversations shows that mutable transcripts can reduce conversation length and eliminate obsolete retained context. These findings provide initial evidence that user-driven revision of conversational history can improve interaction quality and help maintain a more current representation of user intent. The source code and prototype can be accessed at this https URL

</details>


### [63] [DIAL: Position-Debiased LLM Judges with Adaptive Human Preference Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31215)
*Zesheng Cai,Yingqi Fan,Sichang Chen,Jin-Hong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIAL 提出用大量 LLM 比较加少量人类比较来分离评判者位置效应、学习去偏 LLM 偏好并自适应校准到人类偏好，理论覆盖识别性、自适应估计与不确定性量化，并用 21 个 LLM 评判者的 41 万条判断在三个基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为评判者虽然可扩展，但其判断对回答展示顺序敏感，且即使消除位置效应后，仍会系统性偏离人类偏好；论文希望在只有少量人类比较的情况下，结合大量 LLM 比较，得到去偏且对齐人类的偏好排序。

Method: DIAL 是一个统一框架，输入端包括大量 LLM 成对比较（覆盖 21 个 LLM 评判者、两种展示顺序，共 410K 条判断）以及有限的人类成对比较。框架将 LLM 判断中的变异分解为：共享的潜在 LLM 偏好结构、评判者特有效应（包括位置效应）以及人类偏好与 LLM 偏好之间的校准差异。处理流程上，首先通过成对比较在两展示顺序下的观测识别潜在 LLM 偏好与位置效应；随后学习去位置偏差后的 LLM 偏好共享结构，并将其作为锚定信息；再用有限人类比较自适应地校准该结构，使最终偏好估计向人类偏好目标靠拢，同时通过固定权重的不确定性量化给出校准后人类偏好的置信度。理论部分分别研究识别性、自适应估计中 LLM 锚定与人类证据的权衡，以及不确定性量化。实验部分在受控仿真与三个人类偏好基准上分别评估位置去偏效果与人类对齐效果，验证在不平衡顺序下的鲁棒性、有限标签下的人类对齐排序能力以及对 LLM 信息不完美时的自适应能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "位置去偏与人类对齐表现", "value": "在受控仿真和三个人类偏好基准上保持对不平衡回答顺序的鲁棒性，在有限标签下获得较强的人类对齐排序，并在 LLM 信息不完美时向人类证据自适应", "setting": "受控仿真实验与三个人类偏好基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 DIAL 统一框架，将 LLM 比较与有限人类比较结合，分离评判者特有效应、学习去位置偏差的 LLM 偏好共享结构，并自适应校准到人类偏好目标；从理论上分析潜在 LLM 偏好、位置效应与人类校准参数的识别性；提出自适应估计方法，在 LLM 锚定信息与有限人类证据之间进行平衡；给出校准后人类偏好的固定权重不确定性量化方法；构建并发布包含 21 个 LLM 评判者、两种展示顺序、超过 410K 条判断的真实数据资源

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) as a judge enable scalable evaluation, but their judgments can be sensitive to response order and, even after removing such position effects, can still diverge systematically from human this http URL introduce DIAL, a unified framework that combines abundant LLM comparisons with limited human comparisons to separate judge-specific position effects, learn shared structure in position-debiased LLM preferences, and adaptively calibrate that structure toward the human preference target. Theoretically, we study three aspects of DIAL: (i) identification of latent LLM preferences, position effects, and human calibration; (ii) adaptive estimation that balances LLM anchoring against limited human evidence; and (iii) fixed-weight uncertainty quantification for the calibrated human preference. Empirically, we evaluate position debiasing and human alignment separately in controlled simulations and on three human-preference benchmarks, showing that DIAL remains robust to unbalanced response order, achieves strong human-aligned rankings with limited labels, and adapts toward human evidence when LLM information is imperfect. Our real-data study collects over 410K judgments from 21 LLM judges in both display orders, providing a resource for future studies of LLM-judge bias, heterogeneity, and human alignment.

</details>


### [64] [Semantic Navigation for Issue Localization in Code Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31176)
*Yunxiang Wei,Zhenyu Lei,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemNav针对仓库级issue定位提出检索播种+LLM agent证据驱动迭代精化的框架，包含语义导航图、问题条件语义卡与持久候选工作区三个组件，在SWE-bench Lite与PLocBench上把File Hit@10从68.33%提升到82.67%（Gemma 4B），并改善下游问题解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环境难以支撑LLM agent在问题定位中的迭代循环：难以查找未解析的关系目标、需从原始源码重建实体语义、更新候选时缺乏证据依据。

Method: SemNav将问题定位建模为迭代循环：首先用确定性检索生成覆盖较广的候选文件/函数集合作为初始召回；随后由LLM agent持续精化该集合。支撑该循环包含三个模块：Semantic Navigation Graph通过语言服务器按需解析跨文件的程序关系，使agent可直接导航到相关实体，避免在原始源码中搜索未解析的关系目标；Issue-conditioned Semantic Cards为每个实体生成紧凑、基于源码的解释，说明该实体的作用及其与issue的相关性，从而降低上下文负载；Persistent Candidate Workspace持久记录每个候选及其证据依据，使agent能够基于证据进行验证、修订与排序。组件消融与轨迹分析验证三者互补，Semantic Cards相对全文阅读减少48.2%工作上下文。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "File Hit@10", "value": "68.33% -> 82.67%", "setting": "SWE-bench Lite与PLocBench，使用Gemma 4B"}, {"metric": "working-context load", "value": "降低48.2%", "setting": "Semantic Cards相对于full-source reading"}, {"metric": "evidence-quality metrics", "value": "在全部七项指标上排名第一", "setting": "SWE-Explore"}, {"metric": "downstream issue resolution", "value": "44.00% -> 52.33%", "setting": "下游问题解决率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Semantic Navigation Graph：通过语言服务器按需解析程序关系，支持跨文件直接导航到相关实体；Issue-conditioned Semantic Cards：为每个实体提供紧凑、基于源码的语义解释及其与issue的相关性；Persistent Candidate Workspace：持久记录每个候选及其证据依据，支持有据验证、修订与排序

Abstract: Repository-level issue localization aims to identify and rank the files and functions relevant to resolving a reported issue. LLM agents approach this task iteratively: they identify a set of potentially relevant locations, inspect the corresponding code, and revise their judgments about these candidates as new evidence is acquired. Existing environments, however, provide limited support for this loop: agents must search for unresolved relation targets, reconstruct entity semantics from raw source code, and revise candidates without evidential basis. To address these limitations, we present SemNav, a framework that leverages deterministic retrieval to seed a broad candidate set and an LLM agent to continually refine that set, thereby combining initial coverage with evidence-guided revision. SemNav supports this process through three key components. A Semantic Navigation Graph resolves program relations on demand through a language server, enabling direct navigation to related entities across files. Issue-conditioned Semantic Cards provide compact, source-grounded interpretations of each entity's role and relevance to the issue. A persistent Candidate Workspace records each candidate together with its evidential basis, enabling grounded verification, revision, and ranking. Across SWE-bench Lite and PLocBench, SemNav outperforms existing baselines, improving File Hit@10 from 68.33\% to 82.67\% with Gemma 4B. Component ablations and trajectory analysis support the complementary roles of all three components, while Semantic Cards reduce working-context load by 48.2\% relative to full-source reading. SemNav further ranks first on all seven evidence-quality metrics on SWE-Explore and improves downstream issue resolution from 44.00\% to 52.33\%.

</details>


### [65] [Can Linguistic Reasoning Vectors Enhance Multimodal Reasoning Ability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31140)
*Ziyi Wang,Li Li,Aolin Zhou,Yankun Shen,Chonghan Liu,Shuxia Lin,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIFT，一种基于推理向量的轻量级干预方法，将基座 LLM 的语言推理能力迁移到对齐后的 VLM，无需重新训练主干。在六个推理基准、两个 VLM 上验证：基座 LLM 提取的推理向量优于 VLM 自身提取的向量，表明恢复退化推理更应求助于基座 LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态缩放（为预训练 LLM 添加视觉模块并对齐）会退化基座 LLM 原有的语言侧推理能力，且对齐后的 VLM 本身无法可靠访问该能力，如何在不重新训练主干的情况下恢复 VLM 中退化的推理能力。

Method: LIFT 是一种轻量级向量干预方法，用于将推理能力从基座 LLM 迁移到对齐后的 VLM，且不重新训练 VLM 主干。方法定义 Reasoning Vectors 为：在相同输入下，运行两个路径——带有显式推理轨迹的 Reasoner 路径与不带推理轨迹的 Solver 路径——取答案 token 处的隐藏状态差值作为推理向量。这些向量随后被注入目标 VLM 的语言侧激活中。LIFT 还支持可学习的向量适配，即向量本身可训练，而 VLM 主干保持冻结。评估时在匹配协议下分别从基座 LLM 和对齐后的 VLM 提取推理向量进行对比，在六个推理基准上验证从基座 LLM 提取的向量更能恢复退化推理。分析表明注入的向量影响中间推理行为而非仅改变最终答案。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理能力恢复效果对比", "value": "LLM 提取的推理向量一致优于 VLM 提取的推理向量", "setting": "两个 VLM、六个推理基准、匹配协议下的对比实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于向量干预的 LIFT 方法，将基座 LLM 的推理能力迁移到对齐后的 VLM。；定义 Reasoning Vectors：在带有显式推理轨迹的 Reasoner 路径与无推理轨迹的 Solver 路径之间，取答案 token 的隐藏状态差值作为推理向量。；将推理向量注入目标 VLM 的语言侧激活，并支持可学习的向量适配，同时保持 VLM 主干冻结。；在匹配协议下对比基座 LLM 与对齐 VLM 提取的推理向量，实证基座 LLM 是恢复推理能力的更有效来源。；分析表明推理向量影响中间推理行为，而不仅仅是改变最终答案。

Abstract: Most Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are built by extending pretrained Large Language Models (LLMs) with visual modules and multimodal alignment. However, this multimodal scaling often degrades the language-side reasoning ability originally encoded in the base LLM. While the base LLM retains usable reasoning after scaling, the aligned VLM itself cannot reliably access this ability. Therefore, recovering the degraded reasoning capability in VLMs would benefit more from seeking help from the base LLM than from the VLM alone. Motivated by this, we propose LIFT (Language-side reasonIng Facilitation and Transfer), a lightweight vector-intervention method that transfers reasoning capability from the base LLM to the VLM without retraining the backbone. LIFT defines Reasoning Vectors as answer-token hidden-state differences between a Reasoner path with an explicit reasoning trace and a Solver path without it, and injects these vectors into language-side activations of the target VLM. LIFT further supports learnable vector adaptation while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. We evaluate LIFT on two VLMs across six reasoning benchmarks, comparing Reasoning Vectors extracted from the base LLM and from the aligned VLM under matched protocols. Results show that LLM-derived vectors consistently outperform VLM-derived vectors, confirming that the base LLM is a more effective source for recovering reasoning. LIFT partially recovers degraded reasoning through lightweight language-side interventions. Further analyses show that Reasoning Vectors influence intermediate reasoning behavior rather than merely altering final answers. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [66] [Neuralyzing the Trace: Selective Representation-Level Unlearning with Contrastive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31056)
*Itai Zehavi,Fanny Jourdan,Ulrich Aivodji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SCALPEL，一种对比稀疏自编码器，用于细粒度表示级机器遗忘，解决重建式提取中的能量偏置导致的低选择性。理论上证明对比训练促进目标选择性并控制背景扰动，在TOFU上跨Qwen、Llama、Gemma显著优于NMF和标准SAE，与Gradient Difference和RMU相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为层面的遗忘不足以移除狭窄目标（如单个人）的信息，而标准机制可解释性提取器（如重建式稀疏自编码器）因能量偏置偏向主导的背景结构，对目标特定低能量成分选择性差；需要更选择性的表示级干预方法。

Method: SCALPEL为对比稀疏自编码器，用于从大模型内部激活中提取目标选择性的遗忘特征。输入为大模型内部表示（激活），通过稀疏自编码器框架编码到稀疏隐空间。与标准重建式SAE不同，SCALPEL采用对比训练目标：拉近目标相关表示、推远背景表示，从而抑制重建式提取中主导背景结构的能量偏置，使隐特征更聚焦于低能量的目标特定成分。训练时通过对比损失促进目标选择性特征学习，并引入选择分数衡量特征的目标选择性，理论上该分数可控制期望背景知识扰动。推理/干预阶段：利用学得的稀疏特征定位并编辑/抑制目标相关表示，实现表示级遗忘，同时通过选择分数约束减少对非目标知识的扰动。实验中在TOFU上对Qwen、Llama、Gemma模型进行干预评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "unlearning effectiveness/selectivity", "value": "substantially improves over NMF and standard SAE interventions; competitive with Gradient Difference and RMU", "setting": "TOFU benchmark across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别出重建式提取中的能量偏置问题：重建目标偏好主导背景结构而忽略低能量目标特定成分；提出SCALPEL——对比稀疏自编码器，学习更具选择性的遗忘特征；理论证明对比训练促进目标选择性特征，并证明选择分数控制期望背景知识扰动；在TOFU基准上跨Qwen、Llama、Gemma验证，显著优于NMF和标准SAE干预，并与Gradient Difference和RMU具有竞争力

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove targeted information while preserving a model's other abilities. In realistic settings, such as privacy requests under the EU GDPR, the target may be narrow, for example information associated with a single person. Behavioral forgetting alone may be insufficient, motivating interventions directly on internal representations. However, standard mechanistic-interpretability extractors are poorly selective for such targets. We identify an energy bias in reconstruction-based extraction, which favors dominant background structure over low-energy target-specific components. We introduce SCALPEL, a contrastive sparse autoencoder designed to learn more selective forget features. We show theoretically that contrastive training promotes target-selective features and that our selection score controls expected background knowledge perturbation. We validate SCALPEL experimentally on TOFU across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, where it substantially improves over NMF and standard SAE interventions and is competitive with Gradient Difference and RMU, bridging mechanistic interpretability and fine-grained unlearning.

</details>


### [67] [MoMHa: Multi-Objective Optimization of LLM Harnesses over Accuracy, Safety, and Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30967)
*Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Md Mehrab Tanjim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoMHa/Meta-Harness 把 LLM harness(提示构造、调用路由、输出解析代码)当作一等设计面，用 Claude Code 作为具备文件系统访问的 agentic 提议者，对准确率、行为安全与 token 成本三目标做单阶段联合奖励搜索。在 17 个领域、12 模型 4 家族上评估：合成赛道联合均值 0.482 优于 0.198–0.422 的十个基线，真实基准 0.461 优于最强基线 DSPy 的 0.377，U-SafeBench 行为安全复合指标 0.781 最高，且每例比两阶段方案少 95 tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何把 LLM harness(围绕模型的提示构造、调用路由、输出解析代码)当作一等设计面，在准确率、行为安全与 token 成本三个目标间进行多目标优化，而非仅优化准确率。

Method: Meta-Harness 把 harness 视为围绕 LLM 的 Python 代码层，负责构造提示、路由模型调用并解析输出。系统将 harness 设计建模为按领域定义的三目标搜索问题：准确率、行为安全(U-SafeBench 派生的用户特定安全域)与 token 成本。搜索由 agentic 提议者 Claude Code 执行，该提议者拥有完整文件系统访问权限，可读取先前 harness 源码、执行轨迹以及评分产物，据此提出新的 harness 实现。核心配置为单阶段联合奖励提议者 MoMHa，即在同一次搜索中同时优化三个目标的联合奖励；论文将其与两阶段“先准确率后 token”消融、仅标量反馈、仅准确率基线等十种基线对比。评估覆盖 17 个领域：7 个合成能力套件、7 个公开真实基准(HumanEval、MBPP、Spider、FEVER、MMLU-Pro、LawBench、NuminaMath)以及 3 个 U-SafeBench 派生安全域，并在 12 个模型、4 个家族的模型舰队上运行，考察 harness 策略跨模型与跨基准的迁移(12 个目标模型中 8 个无需重训即可迁移)。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "合成赛道联合均值", "value": "0.482(MoMHa)vs 0.198–0.422(十个基线)", "setting": "7 个合成能力套件，12 模型 4 家族,按领域列胜出 7/10"}, {"metric": "真实世界赛道得分", "value": "0.461(MoMHa)vs 0.377(最强基线 DSPy)", "setting": "7 个公开基准(HumanEval、MBPP、Spider、FEVER、MMLU-Pro、LawBench、NuminaMath),按领域列胜出 5/7"}, {"metric": "行为安全复合指标", "value": "0.781", "setting": "U-SafeBench 派生安全域,为所测最高"}, {"metric": "每例 token 用量", "value": "比两阶段替代方案少 95 tokens/例", "setting": "与两阶段“先准确率后 token”方案对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 harness 设计形式化为按领域的三目标(准确率、行为安全、token 成本)搜索问题，由具备完整文件系统访问权限的 agentic 提议者(Claude Code)读取历史 harness 源码、执行轨迹与评分产物来提出新 harness；提出单阶段联合奖励提议者 MoMHa，并证明其优于两阶段“先准确率后 token”消融、仅标量反馈以及仅准确率基线；建立跨 17 个领域(7 个合成能力套件、7 个公开基准、3 个 U-SafeBench 派生用户特定安全域)、12 模型 4 家族的评估协议，并观察到 harness 策略可在 12 个目标模型中的 8 个上无需重训地迁移到未见基准；同时报告行为安全复合指标与 token 成本，把 token 效率作为与安全、准确率并列的显式目标

Abstract: Most work on improving large language models treats accuracy as the sole objective. We argue that the harness, the Python code surrounding the model that constructs prompts, routes calls, and parses outputs, is a first-class design surface whose quality is inherently multi-objective: an accurate harness that refuses no unsafe request, or that consumes an order of magnitude more tokens, is not a good harness. We present Meta-Harness, a system that casts harness design as search over three per-domain objectives (accuracy, behavioural safety, and token cost) solved by an agentic proposer (Claude Code) with full filesystem access to prior harness source, execution traces, and scoring artifacts. Our central finding is that a singlephase joint-reward proposer (MoMHa) outperforms every alternative, including a two-phase "accuracy then tokens" ablation, scalar-only feedback, and an accuracy-only baseline. We evaluate on seventeen domains: seven synthetic capability suites, seven real-world public benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, Spider, FEVER, MMLU-Pro, LawBench, NuminaMath), and three U-SafeBench-derived user-specific safety domains, using a 12-model fleet spanning four families. On the synthetic track MoMHa achieves a joint mean of 0.482 versus 0.198-0.422 for ten baselines, winning $7 / 10$ per-domain columns; on the real-world track it scores 0.461 versus 0.377 for the strongest baseline (DSPy), winning 5/7 columns, demonstrating that harness strategies transfer to unseen benchmarks without retraining on 8 of 12 target models. MoMHa attains the highest measured behavioral safety composite (U-SafeBench, 0.781) and uses 95 fewer tokens per example than the two-phase alternative. We will release all harness code, evaluation infrastructure, and crossmodel logs.

</details>


### [68] [LogicTree-RAG: Logic Tree-guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-form Patent Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30943)
*Jiaqi Zhu,Naili Xing,Hexiang Pan,Haotian Gao,Jianwei Yin,Xiaokui Xiao,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LogicTree-RAG 提出以逻辑树引导的 RAG 框架，用于长篇专利起草。它通过递归证据引导构建层次化技术元素树，并用混合遍历映射到专利章节，实现可控且章节均衡的生成，从而提升内容质量、语言合规性与 token 效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在长篇幅技术文本生成中难以保证全局逻辑一致性与忠实技术推理；专利起草此类任务依赖人工大纲或仅生成局部段落，限制了可扩展的自动化。

Method: LogicTree-RAG 以逻辑树作为全局组织骨架：输入为技术披露内容与相关证据。首先通过证据引导的递归生成构建逻辑树的节点，每个节点代表一个技术元素；该过程不依赖专家定义的大纲先验，而是基于检索到的证据递归扩展树结构。随后，采用混合遍历机制将逻辑树映射到专利的各个章节，确保生成过程可控且各章节内容均衡。生成阶段以逻辑树为条件，逐节点或逐章节地生成文本，从而保持全局逻辑一致性与技术推理的忠实性。论文摘要未披露具体检索器、生成模型规模、训练目标或推理硬件等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出逻辑树（Logic Tree）作为全局组织骨架，无需专家预定义大纲，通过证据引导的递归生成构建层次化技术元素节点。；设计混合遍历机制，将逻辑树映射至专利章节，实现可控且章节均衡的生成。；面向长篇专利起草的端到端 RAG 框架，支持全局一致性与技术推理的忠实性。

Abstract: Long-form technical text generation underpins knowledge-intensive workflows, yet remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) due to the need for globally consistent logical structuring and faithful technical reasoning beyond local coherence. Patent drafting is a canonical instance of this challenge, demanding holistic generation of a legally compliant and technically exhaustive document through sustained multi-expert collaboration. Existing approaches often focus on partial section generation or rely on manually crafted outlines, limiting scalable automation in realistic settings. In this work, we propose LogicTree-RAG, a logic tree-guided retrieval-augmented generation framework that induces a hierarchical logic tree as a global organizational backbone to organize and ground technical disclosures, without relying on expert-defined drafting priors. Each node in the logic tree represents a technical element and is constructed through evidence-guided recursive generation. A hybrid traversal mechanism then maps the logic tree into patent sections, enabling controllable and section-balanced generation. Extensive experiments show that LogicTree-RAG consistently improves content quality and language conformity over strong LLM-based baselines and achieves longer structured generation with high token efficiency, demonstrating the effectiveness of logic-centric generation for complex technical document drafting.

</details>


### [69] [FTB Graph: Determining and Validating First-token Broadcasters and Language-Identity Head Circuits in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30954)
*Arjun Pillai,Christian Hoang,Anjelo Laroza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对六种多语言大模型进行端到端因果电路分析，使用 EAP 和精确激活修补定位首 token 语言身份广播电路，发现 Qwen2.5 base/instruct 电路高度相似，并指出 EAP 线性近似与精确因果干预存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在生成初期如何因果地决定首 token 的目标响应语言，其因果电路尚未被系统性地绘制。

Method: 论文提出一种端到端的结构电路分析流程。输入为多语言提示，目标是定位决定首 token 语言身份的目标响应语言电路。方法采用 Edge Attribution Patching (EAP) 作为归因工具，在 FP16 精度下使用主动钳制计算每条注意力边或 MLP 边的重要性，随后构建驱动首 token 语言广播的有向无环图。为了验证 EAP 发现的电路，作者进一步使用精确激活修补，并设置 2,000 条候选边搜索上限，以控制搜索空间并检验电路必要性。在六种模型架构（GPT-2、BLOOM-560M、Pythia-1B/2.8B、Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct）上提取并比较电路，分析广播枢纽的深度、节点参与度与边预算。同时比较 Qwen2.5-1.5B base 与 instruct 电路的 Jaccard 相似度以评估指令微调的影响，并对比 EAP 分数与精确修补增量，分析线性梯度近似与因果干预之间的差异。推理路径为：对多语言输入进行前向传播，同时利用 EAP 计算边重要性并构建初始电路，再通过精确激活修补进行验证和筛选，最终输出经过验证的首 token 语言广播电路。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用 Edge Attribution Patching 配合 FP16 主动钳制，在六种模型架构上提取驱动首 token 语言广播的有向无环图。；设计精确激活修补验证流程并设置 2,000 条候选边搜索上限，以验证 EAP 发现的电路。；发现不同模型族中存在深层或中深层广播枢纽，并观察到 Pythia-1B 到 2.8B 规模扩大时节点参与增加但验证边预算相似、拓扑更稀疏。；发现 Qwen2.5-1.5B base 与 instruct 电路保留 84.7% Jaccard 相似度，且包含 Layer 27 枢纽，表明首 token 路由主要在预训练阶段建立并因指令微调得以保留。；揭示 EAP 分数与精确修补增量在多数模型中相关性较弱，说明线性梯度近似在 FP16 下可能与因果干预背离，强调精确修补验证的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models operating in multilingual contexts must resolve target response languages early in generation, yet the causal circuitry governing first-token language identity decisions remains poorly mapped. We present an end-to-end structural circuit analysis across six model architectures spanning four families: GPT-2, BLOOM-560M, Pythia-1B/2.8B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B Base/Instruct. Using Edge Attribution Patching (EAP) with FP16 active clamping, followed by exact activation patching verification with a 2,000-candidate-edge search ceiling, we extract directed acyclic graphs driving first-token language broadcasting. Across the standalone models, we observe deep or mid-to-deep broadcasting hubs, though the evidence is strongest for Pythia-2.8B and BLOOM-560M because GPT-2 and Pythia-1B leave few out-of-graph heads for comparison, while both Qwen2.5-1.5B variants invert the necessity check. Scaling from Pythia-1B to 2.8B expands node participation while maintaining a similar verified edge budget, producing sparser topology. The Qwen2.5-1.5B base and instruct circuits retain 84.7% Jaccard similarity, including the Layer 27 hub, indicating that first-token routing is largely established during pretraining and preserved by instruction tuning. Finally, EAP scores correlate weakly with exact patching deltas across most models, showing that linear gradient approximations can diverge from causal interventions in FP16 and motivating exact-patching verification for reliable circuit discovery.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不依赖预训练语言模型、无需针对新图或新标签额外训练分类头/特征投影器的情况下，构建一个可支持任意特征维度与语义、覆盖节点分类、链接预测、节点聚类和图生成等多任务的图基础模型。

Method: 论文摘要仅披露：Acacia 是在 Common Crawl 网页图上从头训练的图基础模型。训练输入为网页图，不依赖预训练 LLM。模型设计上需支持任意特征维度与语义而无需额外训练，并可直接处理节点分类、链接预测、节点聚类与图生成任务，无需添加任务特定分类头或特征投影器。模型具备上下文学习能力。摘要未披露具体模块结构、训练目标、推理路径与数据流细节，因此无法进一步展开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Acacia 图基础模型，直接从 Common Crawl 网页图训练，不依赖预训练 LLM。；支持任意特征维度与语义且无需额外训练，无需为新图或新标签添加分类头或特征投影器。；支持节点分类、链接预测、节点聚类和图生成等多种任务，无需额外训练。；具备上下文学习能力。；提供图模型可以从零训练获得涌现能力的证据。

Abstract: We introduce Acacia, a graph foundation model, trained on the web graph. Acacia (i) supports arbitrary feature dimensionalities and semantics without additional training, (ii) supports a wide range of tasks, including node classification, link prediction, node clustering, and graph generation, without additional training, (iii) has in-context learning capabilities, and (iv) does not rely on pretrained LLMs. In particular, existing graph foundation models often require training additional classification heads or feature projectors to accommodate new graphs or new labels, whereas Acacia does not. Moreover, existing graph foundation models often gain their capabilities by being stitched together with pretrained LLMs, whereas Acacia is trained from scratch using only the Common Crawl web graph. This is also an important result because it provides evidence that graph models can acquire emergent capabilities from scratch like LLMs.

</details>


### [71] [TISD: On-Policy Self-Distillation with Trajectory Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30878)
*Taeckyung Lee,Rinat Amankos,Jeonghye Kim,Hyungjun Yoon,Woogyeol Jin,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TISD 针对 on-policy self-distillation 只能监督学生采样轨迹的瓶颈，提出教师引导的分支-再生成-蒸馏：在分歧峰值处强制教师偏好的分支动作，让学生生成后缀，再用特权教师蒸馏完整轨迹。编码 Avg@4 比 SDPO 高 1.2 点，科学域 Avg@128 等步数高 0.8 点、等时间高 0.3 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation 仅沿学生采样轨迹评估教师目标，当特权教师在已访问前缀偏好另一动作时，只能监督分支决策本身，无法监督该动作后继上下文，造成训练时数据收集瓶颈。

Method: TISD 是训练时的自蒸馏框架，不改变学生模型推理路径。输入为学生模型当前策略在任务上采样的轨迹前缀；核心模块顺序为：(1) 在已访问前缀处使用特权上下文教师计算分布，检测教师-学生分歧峰值；(2) 选择教师偏好的 token 作为分支动作，强制插入该动作形成分支点，而非仅把该 token 作为局部纠正目标；(3) 后缀生成交还学生模型，从而产生由该分支动作诱导的后继上下文；(4) 在特权上下文条件下，用教师对整条分支后轨迹（含分支动作及其后续学生生成）进行蒸馏，提供密集监督。训练目标为最小化学生与教师在特权上下文条件下的分布差异，覆盖分支动作及其后缀，从而让学生不仅学习分支决策，还学习该动作的后继状态。推理时使用学生模型自身策略生成，不依赖教师或干预。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Avg@4", "value": "相比 SDPO 提高 1.2 个百分点", "setting": "编码模型平均"}, {"metric": "Avg@128", "value": "提高 0.8 个百分点", "setting": "科学域平均，等步数预算"}, {"metric": "Avg@128", "value": "提高 0.3 个百分点", "setting": "科学域平均，等时间预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用受控 token 干预诊断教师-学生分歧：发现分歧峰值处教师偏好的 token 可提升学生续写成功率，但其局部纠正价值有限，从而将教师-学生对歧义重新解释为轨迹分支提议。；提出 Trajectory-Intervention Self-Distillation（TISD）：分支-再生成-蒸馏算法，强制采用教师选择的分支动作，将后缀生成交还学生，并在特权上下文条件下由教师蒸馏完整轨迹。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense teacher targets, but evaluates them only along student-sampled rollouts. When the privileged teacher favors an alternative action at a visited prefix, OPSD can provide a target for the branch decision but cannot supervise the successor contexts induced by that action unless the student samples it. This creates a training-time data-collection bottleneck and suggests a different role for teacher-student disagreement: proposing a trajectory branch rather than identifying a sufficient local repair. Our diagnostic framework using controlled token interventions reveals that a teacher-preferred token at peak disagreement can improve student continuation success, while its local corrective value is limited. Motivated by this finding, we introduce a simple branch-regenerate-distill algorithm, Trajectory-Intervention Self-Distillation (TISD). TISD forces a teacher-selected branch action, returns suffix generation to the student, and distills the full trajectory under the privileged-context-conditioned teacher. Across the coding models, TISD improves average Avg@4 over SDPO by 1.2 percentage points. Across the science domains, it improves average Avg@128 by 0.8 points under an equal-step budget and by 0.3 points under an equal-time budget. These results support teacher-guided branching as a way to expose useful successor contexts for self-distillation.

</details>


### [72] [EXAONE Demand 1.0: A Time Series Foundation Model for Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30880)
*Seunghan Lee,Sangjun Han,Jun Seo,Junhyeok Kang,Jaehoon Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EXAONE Demand 1.0 针对通用时间序列基础模型在需求预测上的不足，构建了 1130 万条序列、484 亿观测点、73 个来源的需求专用语料与合成数据，并在冻结骨干上设计四类需求形态的低秩适配分支与基于八个无量纲统计量的路由器。两种训练版本在 22 个留出数据集上均优于 36 个 TSFM，真实数据较纯合成数据有额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用时间序列基础模型的预训练语料中需求序列占比极低，而需求数据具有短历史、频繁零值、缺货截断以及未记录的外生事件等特性，导致现有 TSFM 在需求预测上表现受限。

Method: EXAONE Demand 以通用域时间序列基础模型为冻结骨干网络，不更新其参数。输入端为需求时间序列。适配器部分在冻结骨干上为四类需求形态（平滑、间歇、波动、块状）分别附加低秩分支，每个分支学习对应需求类型的适配表示。路由器读取输入序列的八个无量纲（scale-free）统计量，据此为四个低秩分支输出加权贡献，最终组合成适配后的表示用于预测。训练时仅优化低秩分支与路由器；论文构建了两个版本，分别使用真实与合成需求数据联合训练、以及仅用合成语料训练，以验证真实数据带来的增益。推理路径为：输入序列经统计量计算送入路由器，同时通过冻结骨干与低秩分支得到各分支表示，按路由权重融合后输出需求预测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在 22 个留出数据集上的整体表现", "value": "两个版本均优于 36 个 TSFM", "setting": "22 个留出需求数据集，与 36 个时间序列基础模型对比，未披露具体指标数值"}, {"metric": "真实需求数据带来的增益", "value": "真实与合成数据联合训练优于仅合成数据训练", "setting": "22 个留出数据集对比，未披露具体指标数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建需求专用语料：整合 73 个来源的 1130 万条序列、484 亿个观测点，并引入合成生成器补充公开需求数据中缺失的行为模式。；提出需求感知适配器：在冻结的通用域骨干网络上附加四个低秩分支，分别对应平滑、间歇、波动、块状四类需求序列。；设计路由器：读取输入序列的八个无量纲统计量，动态决定各低秩分支的贡献权重。；训练两种版本：一种在真实与合成需求数据上联合训练，另一种仅在合成语料上训练，验证真实需求数据带来的额外增益。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are pretrained on series from diverse domains, where demand series make up only a small fraction. Demand data has properties that such corpora rarely contain: Short histories, frequent zeros, censoring by stock-outs, and exogenous events that the series does not record. To this end, we propose EXAONE Demand, built on 1) a demand-specific corpus and 2) a demand-aware adapter. For the corpus, we assemble 11.3M series and 48.4B observations from 73 sources, and a synthetic generator supplies the behaviour that open demand data under-represents. For the adapter, we attach low-rank branches to a frozen general-domain backbone, one for each of the four demand classes (smooth, intermittent, erratic, and lumpy), and a router that reads eight scale-free statistics of the input series decides how much each branch contributes. We build EXAONE Demand in two versions, one trained on real-world and synthetic demand together and one trained on the synthetic corpus alone. On 22 held-out datasets, both versions outperform 36 TSFMs, and real-world demand adds a gain over synthetic data alone.

</details>


### [73] [Self-Play Search Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30936)
*Lorenzo Molfetta,Wai-Chung Kwan,Giacomo Frisoni,Luca Ragazzi,Gianluca Moro,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Jeff Z. Pan,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPSD通过自博弈搜索蒸馏生成超人类合成数据,训练LLM推理,在数学基准和游戏胜率上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理能力提升受限于高质量合成数据的稀缺与人工标注成本高。

Method: SPSD框架包含以下模块:1) 在可执行环境(棋盘游戏)中进行自博弈,使用MuZero风格网络;2) 在每个状态,专家网络识别偏好决策、合理备选、合理对手回应和价值估计;3) 将自博弈搜索记录转化为超人类思维链;4) 利用这些数据训练LLM,提供环境接地的监督。推理时,LLM直接生成推理链,无需环境交互。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "六项数学基准平均分", "value": "24.1 到 36.6", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Base"}, {"metric": "留出游戏胜率", "value": "15% 到 45%", "setting": "自博弈训练后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用MuZero风格网络在棋盘游戏上的自博弈生成超人类合成数据；将搜索记录转化为结构化的思维链,提供环境接地监督；标注高效的合成数据生成框架,可迁移到未见过的数学任务

Abstract: Improving reasoning abilities in Large Language Models (LLMs) requires high-quality data that exposes difficult decisions, competing alternatives, and their consequences. Data scarcity is driven by the low quality of synthetic data and the cost of human labeling. We introduce Self-Play Search Distillation (SPSD), a framework for generating superhuman synthetic data via self-play of MuZero-like networks trained on board games. SPSD uses executable environments to turn search into structured reasoning problems. At each state, the expert identifies a preferred decision, plausible alternatives, plausible opponent replies, and value estimates. By converting the self-play search records into superhuman chains-of-thought, we train LLMs with environment-grounded supervision. Although trained only on self-play search records, SPSD transfers to unseen mathematics. On Qwen3-4B-Base, it raises the mean over six mathematics benchmarks from 24.1 to 36.6 while increasing the held-out-game win rate from 15% to 45%. SPSD offers an annotation-efficient way to create high-quality synthetic data for improving LLM performance in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [74] [From Tapping to Hopping: Augmenting Mobile GUI Agents with App-Native Deeplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30887)
*Yuchen Sun,Chenglin Cai,Gongjie Zhang,Tianyu Xia,Quyu Kong,Panrong Tong,Zhengwen Zeng,Long Chen,Steven Hoi,Chongyang Zhang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出混合交互范式，用应用原生deeplink直接导航替代屏幕逐步点击，通过静态分析发现、真机验证与落地页描述构建deeplink目录，并据此实现GUI-Hopper智能体，在真实商业应用中提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体仅靠点击、滑动等GUI动作完成跨界面导航效率低、步骤长、成功率受限，缺乏对应用内直接跳转能力的利用。

Method: 系统分为三个环节。第一，deeplink发现：对目标应用做静态分析，提取候选deeplink。第二，真机验证与落地页描述：在真实设备上实际调用每个候选deeplink，筛选出可用的deeplink，并记录其落地界面描述，形成经过验证且带语义描述的deeplink目录（verified and grounded deeplink catalog）。第三，GUI-Hopper智能体：在交互时查询该目录，选择合适deeplink执行直接导航；对目录无法覆盖的屏幕内操作使用常规GUI动作，并在deeplink失败时回退到GUI动作，从而形成deeplink导航与GUI操作的混合交互流程。论文摘要未披露具体的智能体模型、训练目标、优化方法或推理服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出混合交互（hybrid interaction）范式：用deeplink做直接导航，GUI动作用于屏幕内操作与回退；通过静态分析发现候选deeplink，在真实设备上验证其可用性，并描述其落地页面，构建经过验证且带描述的deeplink目录；基于该目录实现GUI-Hopper智能体，在真实商用应用中提升任务成功率

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents complete tasks using GUI actions like taps and swipes. These actions are broadly applicable across applications, but reaching a navigation interface. A single deeplink call can replace a sequence of screen-by-screen GUI actions. We therefore introduce hybrid interaction, using deeplinks for direct navigation and GUI actions for other on-screen operations and fallback. To enable this, we discover candidate deeplinks through static analysis, validate them on real devices, and describe their observed landing screens. This process creates a verified and grounded deeplink catalog that pairs each working deeplink with a description of its landing screen. Using this catalog, we introduce GUI-Hopper, a improves task success in commercial applications on real devices, further demonstrating the benefits of hybrid interaction.

</details>
